# Cho̤̍ng-sa̤̍ Gi̍
### Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ ē cho̤̍ng-cho̤̍
## 1
1 Kî-táu Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ cho̤̍ng-cho̤̍ ting-dā̤.
2 Dā̤ sī ko̤ng-hṳ, o-bū-bū; ching-e̤ng mi̍ng-dêng a̍ng-a̍ng; Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ ē Síng, eōng-héng leo̍h cûi-mi̍ng.
3 Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ gô̤ng, Da̤u̍h ū gng: cuh ū gng.
4 Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ kua̍ⁿ ca̤̍ gng sī heô: Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ cuh beong gng gah a̍ng.
5 Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ miá ca̤̍ gng ga̤̍u ceo̍ di̍h-dng, miá ca̤̍ a̍ng ga̤̍u ceo̍ má-láu. Ū má-láu, ū di̍h-dng; ca̤̍ sī táu seo̍h di̍h.
6 Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ gô̤ng, Cûi do̤-ng da̤u̍h ū ko̤ng-ki̍, ta̤̍h beong kui siō̤ng hā ē cûi.
7 Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ cuh ceo̍ ko̤ng-ki̍, sâi ko̤ng-ki̍ dêng-ni̍ ē cûi gah ko̤ng-ki̍ ō̤-dâ̤ ē cûi du̍i do̤-ng beong kṳ̍; cuh ū ah-na.
8 Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ miá ca̤̍ ko̤ng-ki̍ ga̤̍u Ting. Ū má-láu, ū di̍h-dng; ca̤̍ sī dā̤-dī di̍h.
9 Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ gô̤ng, Ting-ō̤ ciô̤-sā̤ ē cûi, da̤u̍h cṳ̄ seo̍h ūi-chṳ̍, sâi le̤̍h-dā̤ hēng chōi: cuh ū ah-na.
10 Ca̤̍ do̤ ē tó Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ miá ga̤̍u Le̤̍h-dā̤; miá cûi cṳ̄ ē ūi-chṳ̍ ga̤̍u Hâi: Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ kua̍ⁿ ah-na sī heô.
11 Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ gô̤ng, Dā̤ da̤u̍h saⁿ geh-cî ē châu, gah cha̍i, léng saⁿ go̍ih-cî ē chiu̍; go̍ih-cî bau heo̍h, deo bíng i ē cê̤ng-lūi leo̍h dā̤ lē: cuh ū ah-na.
12 Dā̤ cuh saⁿ geh-cî ē châu, gah cha̍i, gṳ̄-go̤h bíng i ē cê̤ng-lūi, léng geh go̍ih-cî ē chiu̍; go̍ih-cî bau heo̍h, deo bíng i ē cê̤ng-lūi: Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ kua̍ⁿ ah-na sī heô.
13 Ū má-láu, ū di̍h-dng; ca̤̍ sī dā̤-so̤ⁿ di̍h.
14 Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ gô̤ng, Ting-dêng da̤u̍h ū huah-gng ē tâ̤, sâi heong-beh di̍h-dng má-láu, láu ceo̍ gi̍-heō ā̤ diāⁿ seo̍-sí, di̍h-gí, níng-so̍i;
15 bi̍ng-chiâⁿ huah gng leo̍h ting-dêng, muâ ca̤̍u leo̍h dā̤ lē: cuh ū ah-na.
16 Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ ceo̍ nn̄g gá̤ duā gng: duā ē guâng di̍h-dng, sa̤̍ ē guâng má-láu: buh ceo̍ hiô̤-sā̤ ē chaⁿ.
17 Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ bá̤-le̍h i leo̍h ting-dêng, muâ ca̤̍u leo̍h dā̤ lē,
18 ta̤̍h guâng-lî di̍h má, beong gng gah a̍ng: Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ kua̍ⁿ ah-na sī heô.
19 Ū má-láu, ū di̍h-dng; ca̤̍ sī dā̤-si̍ di̍h.
20 Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ gô̤ng, Cûi da̤u̍h muâ saⁿ ū ua̍h-miā ē dō̤ng-beo̍h, buh ū câ̤u boi leo̍h dā̤ sā̤uⁿ, dêng-ni̍ lé̤ ga̍u ko̤ng-ki̍ do̤-ng.
21 Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ cuh ceo̍ duā-hṳ́, léng cûi-lî sê̤ saⁿ ū ua̍h-miā ē dō̤ng-beo̍h, deo bíng i ē cê̤ng-lūi: ga̍u go̤h-cê̤ng boi ē câ̤u, deo bíng i ē cê̤ng-lūi; Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ kua̍ⁿ ah-na sī heô.
22 Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ ce̤h-ho̤h i gô̤ng, Seng-e̤̍h cīng-sā̤, che̤ng-muâ leo̍h hâi; câ̤u iā seng-e̤̍h cīng sā̤ leo̍h dā̤.
23 Ū má-láu, ū di̍h-dng; ca̤̍ sī dā̤-ngō di̍h.
24 Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ gô̤ng, Dā̤ da̤u̍h muâ saⁿ ua̍h ē mo̍ih-nō̤, deo bíng i ē cê̤ng-lūi; táu-saⁿ, táng-hiō̤, iâ-siu̍, deo bíng i ē cê̤ng-lūi: cuh ū ah-na.
25 Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ cuh ceo̍ iâ-siu̍, deo bíng i ē cê̤ng-lūi, táu-saⁿ deo bíng i ē cê̤ng-lūi, dā̤ sā̤uⁿ táng-hiō̤, deo bíng i ē cê̤ng-lūi: Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ kua̍ⁿ ah-na sī heô.
26 Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ gô̤ng, Guâ-go̤h-náng da̤u̍h ceo̍ náng, chā̤uⁿ Guâ-go̤h-náng ē siō̤ng; sâi i guáng hâi lē hṳ́, boi ē câ̤u, táu-saⁿ gah háng dôiⁿ-dāng leo̍h dā̤ ē táng-hiō̤, léng guâng go̤ng-cé̤ⁿ dā̤.
27 Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ cuh bíng beông-sing ē siō̤ng ceo̍ náng, ceo̍ i cuh sī chā̤uⁿ Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ ē siō̤ng; ceo̍ ā dah-bo, iā ceo̍ sîng-ná̤u.
28 Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ ce̤h-ho̤h i; gah i gô̤ng, Seng-e̤̍h cīng-sā̤, che̤ng-muâ leo̍h ciâ dā̤; dā̤ keoh dṳ̂ sê̤ guâng, léng guâng hâi lē ē hṳ́, boi ē câ̤u, gah dā̤-sā̤uⁿ go̤h-cê̤ng ū ua̍h-miā ē mo̍ih-nō̤.
29 Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ gô̤ng, Muâ-dā̤ geh-cî ē cha̍i-se̤, bau-heo̍h ē go̍ih-cî-chiu̍, Guâ deo seo̍ keoh dṳ̂ sia̍h;
30 nō̤ leo̍h dā̤-sā̤uⁿ câu ē iâ-siu̍, ko̤ng-de̤ng boi ē câ̤u, gah dôiⁿ-dāng leo̍h dā̤ go̤h cê̤ng ua̍h ē mo̍ih-nō̤, Guâ deo seo̍ chaⁿ-châu keoh i sia̍h: cuh ū ah-na.
31 Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ kua̍ⁿ sê̤ ceo̍ ē cīng heô. Ū má-láu, ū di̍h-dng; ca̤̍ sī dā̤-la̍h di̍h.
## 2
1 Ting dā̤ māng-beo̍h gi̍-céng siáⁿ lâ̤u.
2 Ga̍u dā̤-chih di̍h, Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ ceo̍ mo̍ih-nō̤ ē gang-hu î-ging é̤ng-ceo̍ng; cuh leo̍h dā̤-chih di̍h ang-hē̤.
3 Ing cî seo̍h di̍h, Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ ceo̍ māng-beo̍h ē gang-hu î-ging é̤ng-ceo̍ng, cuh ang-hē̤; go̍-cheô ga̍ng-ho̤h leo̍h ca̤̍ seo̍h di̍h, diāⁿ ceo̍ Si̍ng-di̍h.
### Ai-déng-hóiⁿ
4 Cho̤̍ng-cho̤̍ ting-dā̤ lái-le̍h sī ah-na; Á̤-Hó̤-Hua Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ ceo̍ ting dā̤ ē di̍h,
5 dā̤ lē bōi saⁿ chô̤-bo̤̍h, bōi saⁿ cha̍i-se̤; ing Á̤-Hó̤-Hua Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ béng leo̍h-hō leo̍h dā̤ lē, iā beó náng geng-ce̤̍ng ca̤̍ dā̤.
6 Ū bó-bū iú dā̤ lē chōi, ta̤̍h ceōng-ce̍h muâ dā̤.
7 Á̤-Hó̤-Hua Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ ē̤ng tó ceo̍ náng, ta̤̍h seng ki̍ beóng di̍h i pi̍-kang, cuh siáⁿ ceo̍ ū seng-síng ē náng.
8 Á̤-Hó̤-Hua Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ leo̍h dang-bing Ai-déng seh seo̍h gá̤ hóiⁿ; ta̤̍h sê̤ ceo̍ ē náng ba̍ng deó hn̄g-lî.
9 Á̤-Hó̤-Hua Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ sâi dā̤ saⁿ go̤h-cêng ē chiu̍, ā̤ kua̍ⁿ lē, ā̤ sia̍h lē; dng hóiⁿ lē do̤-ng ū seng-mīng ē chiu̍, iā ū heong-beh sēng-o̤h ē chiu̍.
10 Ū eó cé̤ng Ai-déng láu chōi, ta̤̍h gua̍ng ca̤̍ hóiⁿ; ca̤̍ eó iú hiô̤ beong ceo̍ si̍ po̤ⁿ.
11 Táu seo̍h dá̤u miá Bî-seo̍ng: kuáng láu leo̍h Ha̍h-hi-la̍h dā̤ ē si̍-úi; hiô̤ dā̤-ho̤ng cheoh-sâng hó̤ng-ging.
12 Ca̤̍ dā̤-ho̤ng ē ging da̍i heô; iā cheoh-sâng bî-do̤-la̍h, (Bî-do̤-la̍h ū náng huang-i̍h ceo̍ beô-co; ū náng huang-i̍h ceo̍ cṳ̂-ha̤uⁿ.) léng so̤h-háng beô-sa̤u̍h. (So̤h-háng beô-sa̤u̍h ū náng huang-i̍h ceo̍ áng-mâ-nô̤; ū náng huang-i̍h ceo̍ chaⁿ-su̍i-ge̤̍h.)
13 Dā̤-dī dá̤u eó miá Gí-heo̍ng; kuáng láu leo̍h Gô-si̍h dā̤ si̍-úi.
14 Dā̤-so̤ⁿ dá̤u có miá Hi-dâ̤-geh, láu leo̍h A̍-seo̍h dang-bing. Dā̤-si̍ dá̤u cuh sī Beh-la̍h eó.
15 Á̤-Hó̤-Hua Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ cuh dua̍ ca̤̍ náng. ba̍ng leo̍h Ai-déng-hóiⁿ, sâi i cai-ce̤̍ng ka̍ng-siû.
16 Á̤-Hó̤-Hua mīng i gô̤ng, Hóiⁿ-lî go̤h chiu̍ ē go̍ih-cî, dṳ̂ ā̤ súi-i̍ meô sia̍h:
17 nō̤ ca̤̍ heong-beh sēng-o̤h-chiu̍ ē go̍ih-cî, dṳ̂ n̄g-tang sia̍h; sia̍h hṳ̂ seo̍h di̍h dih-dih sî.
18 Á̤-Hó̤-Hua Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ gô̤ng, Ca̤̍ náng duaⁿ-gá̤ do̍ beó seo̍h-moi heô, Guâ beoh ceo̍ seo̍h gá̤ po̍i-gâ̤u bang-cē̤ i.
19 Á̤-Hó̤-Hua Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ ta̤̍h tó ceo̍ chéng-iâ go̤h-cê̤ng ē iâ-siu̍, ko̤ng-de̤ng boi ē câ̤u; dua̍ ga̍u A̍-do̤ng mi̍ng-sá̤ⁿ; kua̍ⁿ ca̤̍ náng heō i ga̤̍u seo̍h-moi; ciô̤-sā̤ ū na̍h-miā mo̍ih-nō̤ ē miá, cuh bíng A̍-do̤ng sê̤ ching ē ceo̍ diāⁿ-da̤u̍h.
20 A̍-do̤ng leo̍h le̤̍h-he̤h, boi ē câ̤u, iâ-siu̍, deo heō ā miá; nō̤ A̍-do̤ng beó deh po̍i-gâ̤u bang-cē̤ i.
21 Á̤-Hó̤-Hua Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ sâi A̍-do̤ng heô-keo̍ng, chṳ̂ i seo̍h dá̤u hia̍h-geoh, cuh ta̤̍h ne̤̍h bô muâ hiô̤ ūi-chṳ̍.
22 Á̤-Hó̤-Hua Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ cuh cio̤ng sê̤ ta̤̍h leo̍h A̍-do̤ng sing lē ē hia̍h-geoh ceo̍ ā sîng-ná̤u-náng, dua̍ ga̍u A̍-do̤ng táu-sáⁿ.
23 A̍-do̤ng gô̤ng, Ca̤̍ sī guâ geoh do̤-ng ē geoh, ne̤̍h do̤-ng ē ne̤̍h; ing i sī iú dah-bo-náng sing lē chṳ̂ chōi, da̤u̍h ching i ga̤̍u sîng-ná̤u. (Bing Hi-beh-lái ngói-meóng, dah-bo ga̤̍u I-si, sîng-ná̤u gá̤u I-sa, ca̤̍ nn̄g gu̍ uā, ing ū seo-gṳ̄ng, hêng-míng sîng-ná̤u sī iú dah-bo hiô̤ heō miá ē i̍-gī.)
24 Go̍-cheô náng lī ló̤-bō̤ ná̤u-lâ̤, gah láu-mâ léng-ha̍h, siáⁿ ceo̍ seo̍h tâ̤.
25 Hu-hū nn̄g gá̤ náng, chih-sing lo̍-tâ̤, iā beó sa̤u-lâ̤.

### Náng úi-bōi mīng-līng
## 3

1 Á̤-Hó̤-Hua Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ sê̤ ceo̍ ciô̤-gá̤ seng-beo̍h, beó mo̍ih ā̤ gu-gua̍i leo̍h sió̤. Sió̤ gah sîng-ná̤u-náng gô̤ng, Hóiⁿ-lî ciô̤-gá̤ chiu̍ ē go̍ih-cî, Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ meh deo gîng-ga̍i dṳ̂ n̄g-tang sia̍h lō̤?
2 Sîng-ná̤u-náng i̍ng sió̤ gô̤ng, Hóiⁿ-lî ciô̤-sā̤ chiu̍ ē go̍ih-cî, guâ deo ā̤ sia̍h lē:
3 bí-do̤̍h hóiⁿ do̤-ng ū seo̍h dau chiu̍ ē go̍ih-cî, Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ gîng-ga̍i, gô̤ng, N̄g-tang sia̍h, iā n̄g-tang mo, gaiⁿ lē ā̤ ga̍u sî-mó̤ng.
4 Sió̤ du̍i sîng-ná̤u-náng gô̤ng, Dṳ̂ bōi ih-dēng sî:
5 ing Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ cai dṳ̂ sia̍h ca̤̍ go̍ih-cî hṳ̂ seo̍h di̍h, dṳ̂ ma̍h-ciu ā̤ cing, dṳ̂ cuh chā̤uⁿ Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ ā̤ bēng-be̍h sēng-o̤h, go̍-cheô gîng-ga̍i dṳ̂ n̄g-tang sia̍h.
6 Sîng-ná̤u-náng gi̍ng ca̤̍ chiu̍ ē go̍ih-cî, ā̤ sia̍h, ā̤ kua̍ⁿ lē, buh ā̤ iā di̍-hā̤, tang seo-bō, cuh dia̍ng ca̤̍ go̍ih-cî sia̍h: buh ta̤̍h keoh i dah-bo, i dah-bo iā sia̍h.
7 Nn̄g gá̤ náng ē ma̍h-ciu cuh cing, cia̍ⁿ gi̍ng-gah sī chih-sing lo̍-tâ̤; cuh bīng bú-hua-gô̤ ē chiu̍-na̤u̍h ceo̍ geóng.
8 Ga̍u di̍h-táu siá, ná̤u ē boiⁿ kî, Á̤-Hó̤-Hua Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ giáⁿ leo̍h hóiⁿ-lî: A̍-do̤ng gah sîng-ná̤u-náng tiaⁿ-mói Á̤-Hó̤-Hua Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ ē siaⁿ-ing, cuh deó leo̍h hóiⁿ ē chiu̍-pa̍ng, piā Á̤-Hó̤-Hua Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ ē mi̍ng.
9 Á̤-Hó̤-Hua Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ ga̤̍u A̍-do̤ng gô̤ng, Dṳ̂ deó deoh-leo? 
10 I i̍ng gô̤ng, Guâ tiaⁿ-mói Dṳ̂ siaⁿ-ing deó hóiⁿ-lî, ing guâ lo̍-tâ̤ ē é̤ng-go̍, cuh giaⁿ, câu kṳ̍ deó.
11 Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ gô̤ng, Hn̍g-náng gah dṳ̂ gô̤ng, dṳ̂ lo̍-tâ̤ lē? Guâ gi̍ng-cî dṳ̂ n̄g-tang sia̍h ca̤̍ chiu̍ ē go̍ih-cî, dṳ̂ ū sia̍h lō̤?
12 A̍-do̤ng gô̤ng, Dṳ̂ sê̤ seo̍ guâ ē sîng-ná̤u-náng, ta̤̍h ca̤̍ chiu̍ ē go̍ih-cî keoh guâ, guâ cuh sia̍h.
13 Á̤-Hó̤-Hua Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ cuh ka̍ng sîng-ná̤u-náng gô̤ng, Dṳ̂ seh-na ah-na ceo̍ lē? Sîng-ná̤u-náng i̍ng gô̤ng, Sī sió̤ iû-he̍h guâ, guâ cia̍ⁿ sia̍h.

### Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ ē se̤ng-pua̍ng
14 Á̤-Hó̤-Hua Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ cuh gah sió̤ gô̤ng, Dṳ̂ gi̍-céng ceo̍ ca̤̍ seō, bih-dēng piāⁿ hiô̤-sā̤ le̤̍h-he̤h, beh-siu̍, ge̍ng-ga siū mo̤̍; dṳ̂ da̤u̍h ē̤ng bah-dô giáⁿ-dō, seo̍h-sa̤̍ sia̍h tó.
15 Guâ beoh sâi dṳ̂ gah sîng-ná̤u-náng geh-giú, dṳ̂ ē hā̤u-a̤̍ gah i ē hā̤u-a̤̍ iā geh-giú: i hā̤u-a̤̍ beoh pā sio̤ng dṳ̂ ē táu, dṳ̂ beoh pā sio̤ng i ē ko̤-āu-daⁿ.
16 Buh gah sîng-ná̤u-náng gô̤ng, Guâ dih-dih sâi dṳ̂ dua̍-sing cīng-kô, saⁿ-giô̤ⁿ keh-kui; dṳ̂ dih-dih siô̤ng-bō dṳ̂ dah-bo, dah-bo iā guâng dṳ̂.
17 Buh gah A̍-do̤ng gô̤ng, Dṳ̂ gi̍-céng tiaⁿ lāu-mâ ē uā, sia̍h Guâ sê̤ gi̍ng-cî sia̍h ē go̍ih-cî; dā̤ dih-dih ing dṳ̂ siū mo̤̍; dṳ̂ dih-dih seo̍h-sa̤̍-náng ló̤-kô, cia̍ⁿ ā̤ sia̍h ca̤̍ dā̤ lē sê̤ cheoh-sâng ē mo̍ih-nō̤:
18 dā̤ dih-dih ing-ūi dṳ̂ saⁿ chi̍-châu cīng sā̤; dṳ̂ dih-dih sia̍h chéng ē cha̍i:
19 ih-dēng guāⁿ láu muâ-mi̍ng, cia̍ⁿ ā̤ deh dih sia̍h, di̍h-di̍h gui ga̍u dṳ̂ sê̤ cheoh ē tó lē; dṳ̂ beông-lái sī tó, dih-dih ca̍i gui tó lē dôiⁿ.
20 A̍-do̤ng miá i ē láu-mâ ga̤̍u Hā-hua; (Hā-hua huang-i̍h cuh sī saⁿ ē i̍-gī) ing i sī ló̤ng-cô̤ng bā-sa̍ⁿ ē ná̤u-lâ̤.
21 Á̤-Hó̤-Hua Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ ta̤̍ A̍-do̤ng léng i lāu-mâ ceo̍ pói ē i-ná̤u keoh sē̤ng.

### Keoh de̤̍h cheoh Ai-déng-hóiⁿ
22 Á̤-Hó̤-Hua Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ gô̤ng, Náng î-ging ā̤ bēng-be̍h sēng-o̤h, hô̤ng-heoh chā̤uⁿ Guâ-go̤h-náng; giaⁿ lē i cheong-chiû, iā dia̍ng seng-mīng-chiu̍ ē go̍ih-cî sia̍h, cuh ā̤ ṳ̂ng-seng:
23 go̍-cheô Á̤-Hó̤-Hua Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ sâi ca̤̍ náng cheoh Ai-déng-hóiⁿ, geng-ce̤̍ng i-ga̍i sê̤ cheoh ē tó.
24 cuh de̤̍h ca̤̍ náng cheoh kṳ̍; leo̍h Ai-déng-hóiⁿ dang-bing seh Gi-lō-bing, gah ā̤ ga̍i-bû ē lī-gia̍ng, ta̤̍h bô̤-siû seng-mīng-chiu̍ ē dō.

## 4 
1 A̍-do̤ng gah lāu-mâ Hā-hua ceo̍ seo̍h báng-ga̤ⁿ; cuh dua̍-sing saⁿ Gai-ṳ̂ng, (Gai-ṳ̂ng huang-i̍h cuh sī "Deh da̤u̍h" ē i̍-gī) gô̤ng, Cṳ̂ sâi guâ deh dah-bo-giô̤ⁿ.
2 Buh saⁿ Gai-ṳ̂ng i-dī A̍-beh. A̍-beh sī n̍g-á̤uⁿ ē náng, Gai-ṳ̂ng sī ceo̍-chéng ē náng.
3 Ū seo̍h di̍h, Gai-ṳ̂ng ta̤̍h tó-sâng he̤̍ng keoh Cṳ̂.
4 A̍-beh ta̤̍h táu seo̍h tai saⁿ ē á̤uⁿ, léng ca̤̍ á̤uⁿ-iú lí he̤̍ng; Cṳ̂ ka̍ng-go̍ Â-beh léng i sê̤ he̤̍ng ē mo̍ih-nō̤:
5 beó ka̍ng-go̍ Gai-ṳ̂ng léng i sê̤ he̤̍ng ē mo̍ih-nō̤. Gai-ṳ̂ng cuh bi̍ng-mi̍ng duā siū-ki̍.
6 Cṳ̂ gah Gai-ṳ̂ng gô̤ng, Dṳ̂ bi̍ng-mi̍ng siū-ki̍ seo̍h-moi é̤ng-go̍ lē?
7 Seh-seô dṳ̂ héng-sēng, meh bā̤ gi̍ng siū-da̍h lō̤? Dû nō̤ héng-o̤h, cōi-gia̍h cuh ho̤̍h leo̍h móiⁿ-sá̤ⁿ; i iā dih-dih siô̤ng-bō dṳ̂, dṳ̂ ho̤h beoh guâng i.

### Gai-ṳ̂ng gah A̍-beh
8 Î-hā̤u Gai-ṳ̂ng gah i-dī A̍-beh seo̍h ca̤̍ lái-le̍h; nn̄g gá̤ náng leo̍h chéng do̤-ng, Gai-ṳ̂ng cuh bā-î pā i-dī A̍-beh, tái i.
9 Cṳ̂ ka̍ng Gai-ṳ̂ng gô̤ng, Dṳ̂-di A̍-beh deó deoh-leo? Gai-ṳ̂ng i̍ng gô̤ng, N̄g-cai: guâ meh sī ka̍ng-guâng guâ-dī ē náng lō̤?
10 Á̤-Hó̤-Hua gô̤ng, Dṳ̂ ū ceo̍ seo̍h-moi? dṳ̂-dī ē hā̤ ū siaⁿ-ing iú dā̤ lē huā giú Guâ.
11 Dā̤ koi chu̍i, iú dṳ̂ chiû lē cī dṳ̂-dī ē hā̤; dṳ dih-dih siū mo̤̍, bā̤ deh ca̤̍ dā̤ ē lī-ih.
12 Dṳ̂ geng-ce̤̍ng dā̤, dā̤ n̄g ca̍i ta̤̍ dṳ̂ cheoh-li̍h; dṳ̂ dih-dih liú-dō̤ng leo̍h dā̤-sā̤uⁿ.
13 Gai-ṳ̂ng du̍i Cṳ̂ gô̤ng, Guâ siū diāⁿ-cōi cīng dāng, si̍h-si̍h bā̤ dng-dih kî.
14 Cṳ̂ da de̤̍h Guâ lī ca̤̍ dā̤: n̄g é̤ng guâ ca̍i gi̍ng Cṳ̂ ē mi̍ng: guâ gi̍-céng pa̤u-liú leo̍h dā̤-sā̤uⁿ, háng náng gṳ̄-da̤u̍h guâ, dih-dih tái guâ.
15 Cṳ̂ cuh gah Gai-ṳ̂ng gô̤ng, Háng tái Gai-ṳ̂ng ē náng dih-dih siū hua̍h chih bōi. Cṳ̂ cuh ta̤̍h gi̍-heō seo̍ keoh Gai-ṳ̂ng, mêng-dih náng seo̍h gṳ̄-da̤u̍h cuh tái i.
16 Iú ciâ Gai-ṳ̂ng te̤̍, lī Cṳ̂ mi̍ng-sá̤ⁿ, kṳ̍ Ai-déng dang-bing, Nô̤-deh dā̤-ho̤ng do̍.

### Gai-ṳ̂ng ē hā̤u-dāi
17 Gai-ṳ̂ng gah i lāu-mâ ceo̍ seo̍h báng; i lāu-mâ cuh dua̍-sing saⁿ Î-do̤̍h: hṳ̂ seo̍h sí Gai-ṳ̂ng kî seo̍h-ceō siáⁿ, cuh ta̤̍h i giô̤ⁿ ē miá heō ca̤̍ siáⁿ ga̤̍u Î-do̤̍h.
18 Î-do̤̍h saⁿ Î-la̍h; Î-la̍h saⁿ Bî-hō-ngâ-lī; Bî-hō-ngâ-lī saⁿ Mâ-tô-sah-lī; Mâ-tô-sah-lī saⁿ La̍h-be̍h.
19 La̍h-be̍h teô nn̄g gá̤ lāu-mâ; seo̍h gá̤ miá A̍-dāi, seo̍h gá̤ miá Sêng-la̍h.
20 A̍-dāi saⁿ Ngâ-bah: i sī do̍ da̤̍uⁿ-báng n̍g táu-saⁿ náng ē cô-co̤ng.
21 I-dī miá Iú-bah: sī duáⁿ-gíng choi-sa̤u náng ē cô-co̤ng.
22 Sêng-la̍h saⁿ Tô-bah-gai-ṳ̂ng: sī pā-dáng pā-tī sai-hū ē cô-co̤ng; Tô-bah-gai-ṳ̂ng ē i-mo̍i miá Ná-mâ.
23 La̍h-be̍h gah i nn̄g gá̤ lāu-mâ gô̤ng:
A̍-dāi Sêng-la̍h da̤u̍h tiaⁿ guâ ē siaⁿ-íng;
La̍h-be̍h ē lāu-mâ tiaⁿ guâ ē uā:
Co̤̍ng-seō sio̤ng guâ, guâ beoh tái i;
Iu̍-dó̤ng hāi guâ, guâ beoh hāi i:
24 Seh-seô tái Gai-ṳ̂ng dih-dih siū hua̍h chih bōi,
Nō̤ tái La̍h-be̍h, dih-dih siū hua̍h chih-se̍h-chih bōi.
25 A̍-do̤ng buh gah i lāu-mâ ceo̍ seo̍h báng; i lāu-mâ saⁿ giô̤ⁿ, miá i ga̤̍u Seh-de̍h. (Seh-de̍h huang-i̍h cuh sī seh-li̍h ē i̍-gī.) gô̤ng, Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ ing-ūi guâ, li̍h seo̍h gá̤ giô̤ⁿ, ta̤̍ Gai-ṳ̂ng sê̤ tái ē A̍-beh.
26 Seh-de̍h iā saⁿ giô̤ⁿ, miá i ga̤̍u Î-nô̤-seō: hiô̤ sí-cā̤ náng kî-chiû dô̤-go̤̍ Á̤-Hó̤-Hua ē miá. (He̍h ceo̍, Náng kî-chiû cia̍ⁿ ching Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ ga̤̍u Á̤-Hó̤-Hua.)

### A̍-do̤ng ē hā̤u-dāi (Dāi-siō̤ng 1:1-4)
## 5
1 A̍-do̤ng ē hāu-a̤̍ io̤h-lio̤̍h gi̍ deó ō̤-dâ̤. Dng Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ cho̤̍ náng sí-cā̤, sī chā̤uⁿ Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ ē siō̤ng cho̤̍ i;
2 cho̤̍ náng, iā cho̤̍ dṳ̂; dng cho̤̍ i bṳ̂ seo̍h di̍h, Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ ta̤̍h ho̤h-ki̍ ṳ̂ng i, ching i ceo̍ náng.
3 A̍-do̤ng seo̍h-bā so̤ⁿ-se̍h ho̍i buh saⁿ giô̤ⁿ, ching-chā̤uⁿ ga̍i ē siō̤ng, ching i ē miá ga̤̍u Seh-de̍h.
4 A̍-do̤ng saⁿ Seh-de̍h āu, cāi sa̤̍ bā̤-bā níng, ho̤h ca̍i saⁿ náng-dṳ̂-giô̤ⁿ.
5 A̍-do̤ng ho̍i-siū sia̍h ga̍u gâu-bā so̤ⁿ-se̍h ho̍i cia̍ⁿ sî.
6 Seh-de̍h seo̍h-bā léng ngō ho̍i saⁿ Î-nô̤-seō:
7 Seh-de̍h saⁿ Î-nô̤-seō āu, cāi sa̤̍ bā̤-bā léng chih níng, ho̤h ca̍i saⁿ náng-dṳ̂-giô̤ⁿ:
8 Seh-de̍h ho̍i-siū sia̍h ga̍u gâu-bā se̍h-dī ho̍i cia̍ⁿ sî.
9 Î-nô̤-seō gâu-se̍h ho̍i saⁿ Gai-náng:
10 Î-nô̤-seō saⁿ Gai-náng āu, cāi sa̤̍ bā̤-bā se̍h-ngō níng, ho̤h ca̍i saⁿ náng-dṳ̂-giô̤ⁿ:
11 Î-nô̤-seō ho̍i-siū sia̍h ga̍u gâu-bā léng ngō ho̍i cia̍ⁿ sî.
12 Gai-náng chih-se̍h ho̍i saⁿ Mâ-leh-le̍h:
13 Gai-náng saⁿ Mâ-leh-le̍h āu, cāi sa̤̍ bā̤-bā si̍-se̍h níng, ho̤h ca̍i saⁿ náng-dṳ̂-giô̤ⁿ:
14 Gai-náng ho̍i-siū sia̍h ga̍u gâu-bā ih-se̍h ho̍i cia̍ⁿ sî.
15 Mâ-leh-le̍h la̍h-se̍h-ngō ho̍i saⁿ Ngâ-le̍h:
16 Mâ-leh-le̍h saⁿ Ngâ-le̍h āu, cāi sa̤̍ bā̤-bā so̤ⁿ-se̍h níng, ho̤h ca̍i saⁿ náng-dṳ̂-giô̤ⁿ.
17 Mâ-leh-le̍h ho̍i-siū sia̍h ga̍u bā̤-bā gâu-se̍h-ngō ho̍i cia̍ⁿ sî.
18 Ngâ-le̍h seo̍h-bā la̍h-se̍h-dī ho̍i saⁿ Î-do̤̍h:
19 Ngâ-le̍h saⁿ Î-do̤̍h āu, cāi sa̤̍ bā̤-bā níng, ho̤h ca̍i saⁿ náng-dṳ̂-giô̤ⁿ:
20 Ngâ-le̍h ho̍i-siū sia̍h ga̍u gâu-bā la̍h-se̍h-dī ho̍i cia̍ⁿ sî.
21 Î-do̤̍h la̍h-se̍h-ngō ho̍i, saⁿ Mâ-tô-sah-la̍h:
22 Î-do̤̍h saⁿ Mâ-tô-sah-la̍h āu, hō̤ng-seō Siō̤ng-Da̤̍; ("Hō̤ng-seō Siō̤ng-Da̤̍," ngé̤ng-meóng sī "Gah Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ cá̤ giáⁿ.") cāi sa̤̍ so̤ⁿ bā níng, ho̤h ca̍i saⁿ náng-dṳ̂-giô̤ⁿ:
23 Î-do̤̍h ho̍i-siū sia̍h ga̍u so̤ⁿ-bā la̍h-se̍h-ngō ho̍i:
24 Î-do̤̍h hō̤ng-seō Siō̤ng-Da̤̍, Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ cī i kṳ̍; go̍-cheô beó deó ciâ sa̤̍-gang.
25 Mâ-tô-sah-la̍h seo̍h-bā beh-se̍h-chih ho̍i saⁿ La̍h-be̍h:
26 Mâ-tô-sah-la̍h saⁿ La̍h-be̍h āu, cāi sa̤̍ chih-bā beh-se̍h-dī níng, ho̤h ca̍i saⁿ náng-dṳ̂-giô̤ⁿ:
27 Mâ-tô-sah-la̍h ho̍i-siū sia̍h ga̍u gâu-bā la̍h-se̍h-gâu ho̍i cia̍ⁿ sî.
28 La̍h-be̍h seo̍h-bā beh-se̍h-dī ho̍i saⁿ giô̤ⁿ:
29 ching i miá ga̤̍u Nô̤-a̍, (Nô̤-a̍ huang-i̍h cuh sī "ang-hē̤" ē i̍-gī) gô̤ng, Cṳ̂ lâ̤-mo̤̍ ciâ dā̤, sâi guâ chiû ceo̍ ló̤-kô; da ca̤̍ giô̤ⁿ dih-dih ang-hu̍i guâ.
30 La̍h-be̍h saⁿ Nô̤-a̍ āu, cāi sa̤̍ ngō-bā gâu-se̍h-ngō níng, ho̤h ca̍i saⁿ náng-dṳ̂-giô̤ⁿ.
31 La̍h-be̍h ho̍i-siū sia̍h ga̍u chih-bā chih-se̍h-chih ho̍i cia̍ⁿ sî.
32 Nô̤-a̍ ngō-bā ho̍i, saⁿ Siâng, Háng, Ngâ-heo̍h.

### Náng ē cōi-o̤h
## 6
1 Náng hing-ō̤ng leo̍h dā̤-sā̤uⁿ, iā ū saⁿ sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ,
2 Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ ē giô̤ⁿ gi̍ng sa̤̍-gang náng ē sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ é̤ng-bāu bā̤u-li̍; cuh bíng i sê̤ chṳ̂-i̍ ē, teô lí ceo̍ lāu-mâ.
3 Cṳ̂ gô̤ng, Náng gi̍-céng sī ne̤̍h-tâ̤, Guâ ē Síng bā̤ ṳ̂ng-ê̤ng gṳ-cṳ̄ i lī-li̍, (He̍h ceo̍ "Guâ ē Síng beó ṳ̂ng-ê̤ng gah i seo-caⁿ") nō̤ meô é̤ng i ga̍u seo̍h-bā dī-se̍h níng. (He̍h ceo̍ "Î-hā̤u i ē ho̍i-siū gah miā ga̍u seo̍h-bā dī-se̍h níng")
4 Do̤ng-sí ū gá̤ⁿ-duā ē náng deó sa̤̍-sā̤uⁿ, î-hā̤u Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ ē giô̤ⁿ gah ca̤̍ náng ē sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ ceo̍ seo̍h báng, saⁿ giô̤ⁿ iā gá̤ⁿ-duā cuh sī siō̤ng-gô ga̍u ciâ ing-hé̤ng cheoh-miá ē náng.
5 Cṳ̂ gi̍ng sa̤̍-sā̤uⁿ ē náng cōi-o̤h gua̍ng-íng, sing lē sê̤ pā-sua̍ⁿ ē, sí-sió̤ng sī gái.
6 Leo̍h ciâ Cṳ̂ tê̤-hôi ceo̍ náng leo̍h sa̤̍-sā̤uⁿ, sing-lî cīng kô.
7 Cṳ̂ gô̤ng, Guâ sê̤ ceo̍ ē náng. beoh be̍h i leo̍h dā̤-sā̤uⁿ; cuh sī náng gah táu-saⁿ, táng-hiō̤, ting lē ē câ̤u; ing Guâ ceo̍ i, da te̤̍-hôi.
8 Nō̤ Nô̤-a̍ mó̤ng-ṳng leo̍h Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ mi̍ng-sá̤ⁿ.

### Nô̤-a̍
9 Nô̤-a̍ ē lái-le̍h gi̍ deó ō̤-dâ̤. Nô̤-a̍ leo̍h hṳ̂ seo̍h dāi náng do̤-ng sī gī-cíng, é̤ng-cíng, hō̤ng-seō Siō̤ng-Da̤̍.
10 Nô̤-a̍ saⁿ so̤ⁿ gá̤ giô̤ⁿ, cuh sī Siâng, Háng, Ngâ-heo̍h.
11 To̤ng sa̤̍-gang u̍-o̍i leo̍h Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ mi̍ng-sá̤ⁿ, muâ ceo̍ gió̤ng-bō̤.
12 Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ kua̍ⁿ sa̤̍-gang u̍-o̍i; cāi dā̤ háng ū hā̤-ki̍ ē, sê̤ ceo̍ deo sī oi-siá.
13 Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ gah Nô̤-a̍ gô̤ng, Háng ū hā̤-ki̍ ē deo beoh siū Guâ dṳ́-be̍h; ing i gió̤ng-bō̤ muâ ceo̍; Guâ beoh be̍h i leo̍h dā̤-sā̤uⁿ.
14 Dṳ̂ da da̤u̍h ē̤ng go̤-hi-chó̤ (Go̤-hi-chó̤, ū náng huang-i̍h ceo̍ sé̤ng-bā-chó̤, iā ū huang-i̍h ceo̍ pú-chó̤) ceo̍ seo̍h dá̤u duā-seóng; lī-li̍ co̤̍h ā báng-ga̤ⁿ, ta̤̍h bā-au dó ca̤̍ seóng ē lī-guā.
15 Dṳ̂ ceo̍ ca̤̍ seóng, ā̤uⁿ-sih sī ah-na: dńg so̤ⁿ-bā chiû-daⁿ, (Seo̍h chiû-daⁿ io̤h-lio̤̍h sī chā̤uⁿ-bua̍ⁿ.) kuā ngō-se̍h chiû-daⁿ, gá̤ⁿ so̤ⁿ-se̍h chiû-daⁿ.
16 Seóng-dêng ceo̍ ā te̤ng, gá̤ⁿ seo̍h chiû-daⁿ, seóng-bing ba̍ng ā mói; beong siō̤ng, dé̤ng, hā, so̤ⁿ ceng.
17 Guâ ih-dēng ē̤ng duā-cûi ci̍ng ca̤̍ dā̤, dṳ́-be̍h ting-ō̤ lô̤ng-cô̤ng ū hā̤-ki̍ ē mo̍ih-nō̤; háng deó dā̤-sā̤uⁿ ē, dih-dih be̍h-mó̤ng.
18 Guâ gah dṳ́ li̍h-io̤h; dṳ́ da̤u̍h chio̤̍ dṳ̂ bô, giô̤ⁿ, sing-mū, deo sā̤uⁿ ca̤̍ seóng.
19 Dṳ̂ cio̤ng go̤h-cê̤ng ua̍h ē mo̍ih-nō̤, bôi cê̤ng gṳ̄-go̤h nn̄g táu, cuh sī seo̍h gang seo̍h beô; dua̍ i sā̤uⁿ seóng, gah dṳ̂ cá̤ bô̤-cé̤ng ua̍h-miā.
20 Boi ē câ̤u gṳ̄-go̤h bíng i cê̤ng-lūi, câu ē iâ-siu̍ gṳ̄-go̤h bíng i cê̤ng-lūi, gah bó̤ dā̤-lō̤ táng-hiō̤ gṳ̄-go̤h bíng i cê̤ng-lūi, bôi lūi gṳ̄-go̤h nn̄g táu, gah dṳ̂ cá̤ sā̤uⁿ seóng, bô̤-cé̤ng i ē ua̍h-miā.
21 Dṳ̂ da̤u̍h có̤ng-cih lô̤ng-cô̤ng sia̍h ē mo̍ih-nō̤, ta̤̍ dṳ̂ léng ciô̤-sā̤ lūi ceo̍ ná̤u-châu.
22 Nô̤-a̍ deo bíng Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ ē mīng-līng kṳ̍ héng.

### Duā-cûi
## 7
1 Cṳ̂ heong-nu̍ Nô̤-a̍ gô̤ng, Guâ leo̍h ciâ sa̤̍-ga̍i, nō̤ kua̍ⁿ dṳ̂ héng-gī deó Guâ mi̍ng-sá̤ⁿ; dṳ̂ da gah dṳ̂ seo̍h cho̍ náng, tang sā̤uⁿ duā-seóng.
2 Háng táu-saⁿ tā-gā̤ ē, gang ē chih táu, beô ē chih táu; beó tā-gā̤ ē lūi, gang beô gṳ̄-go̤h seo̍h táu;
3 boi ē câ̤u, iā dua̍ gang chih táu, beô chih táu: ta̤̍h láu ca̤̍ cê̤ng-lūi leo̍h dā̤-sā̤uⁿ.
4 Geo̍ chih di̍h, Guâ beoh ga̍ng duā-hō leo̍h dā̤-sā̤uⁿ si̍-se̍h di̍h, si̍-se̍h má; ta̤̍h sê̤ ceo̍ ciô̤-gá̤ ua̍h ē mo̍ih-nō̤, beoh deo be̍h leo̍h dā̤-sā̤uⁿ.
5 Nô̤-a̍ cuh deo bíng Cṳ̂ sê̤ mīng ē kṳ̍ héng.
6 Duā-cûi ci̍ng ca̤̍ dā̤ sí-cā̤, Nô̤-a̍ la̍h-bā ho̍i leō.
7 Nô̤-a̍ gah bô giô̤ⁿ léng sing-mū deo sā̤uⁿ kṳ̍ duā-seóng, piā ca̤̍ duā-cûi.
8 Ching-geh ē táu-saⁿ gah beó ching-geh ē táu-saⁿ, bôi ē câ̤u, gah dôiⁿ-dāng dā̤-lō̤ go̤h-cê̤ng ē tang-hiō̤,
9 beô gang seo-po̍i ga̍u, do̍i Nô̤-a̍ di̍h kṳ̍ duā-seóng lē, bíng Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ sê̤ mīng Nô̤-a̍ ē.
10 Chih di̍h āu, duā-cûi ci̍ng ciâ dā̤.
11 Dû-dû Nô̤-a̍ la̍h-bā ho̍i, dī-go̍ih se̍h-chih di̍h; hṳ̂ seo̍h di̍h duā-hâi ē sió̤ⁿ-táu deo e̤̍ng chōi, ting iā pua̍ i ē pa̍ng.
12 Hō leo̍h dā̤-sā̤uⁿ si̍-se̍h di̍h, si̍-se̍h má.
13 Dng hṳ̂ seo̍h di̍h, Nô̤-a̍ gah so̤ⁿ gá̤ giô̤ⁿ, Siâng, Háng, Ngâ-heo̍h; Nô̤-a̍ ē lāu-mâ gah so̤ⁿ gá̤ sing-mū, deo di̍h kṳ̍ duā-seóng lē.
14 I-go̤h-náng gah beh-siu̍ bíng i ē cê̤ng-lūi, le̤̍h-he̤h bíng i ē cê̤ng-lūi, dôiⁿ-dāng leo̍h dā̤-lō̤ ē táng-hiō̤, bíng i ē cê̤ng-lūi, ciô̤-sā̤ hi-nâ̤u, bíng i ē cê̤ng-lūi.
15 Háng ū hā̤-ma̍h ū seng-ki̍ ē, gṳ̄-go̤h ū po̍i-du̍i, do̍i Nô̤-a̍ sā̤uⁿ seóng.
16 Háng sā̤uⁿ seóng ū hā̤-ki̍ ē mo̍ih-nō̤, deo sī bíng Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ sê̤ mīng Nô̤-a̍ ē, ū gang ū beô: Cṳ̂ cuh ta̤̍ i goiⁿ seóng-mói.
17 Duā-cûi ci̍ng ca̤̍ dā̤ si̍-se̍h di̍h; cûi-sa̤̍ cīng duā, sâi seóng kî pú leo̍h dā̤-sā̤uⁿ.
18 Cûi-sa̤̍ leo̍h dā̤-sā̤uⁿ meô dn̂g meô gá̤ⁿ; seóng leo̍h cûi-mi̍ng pú lí pú kṳ̍.
19 Cûi-sa̤̍ leo̍h dā̤-sā̤uⁿ dn̂g cīng gá̤ⁿ; to̤ng ting-ō̤ gá̤ⁿ-gá̤ⁿ, ē suaⁿ deo keoh ing kṳ̍.
20 Cûi-sa̤̍ duā dn̂g, ā̤ gá̤ⁿ go̤h suaⁿ se̍h-ngō chiû-daⁿ; suaⁿ-niâ deo ing kṳ̍.
21 Dā̤-sā̤uⁿ go̤h-lūi ua̍h ē mo̍ih-nō̤, boi ē câ̤u, táu-saⁿ, iâ-siu̍, gah dôiⁿ-dāng dā̤-lō̤ ē táng-hiō̤, léng náng-lui, deo ci̍ng sî kṳ̍. 
22 Háng deó do̤-dā̤ lē, ū pi̍ ā̤ tang-ku̍i ē, ló̤ng-cô̤ng deo sî-mó̤ng.
23 Dā̤-sā̤uⁿ ló̤ng-cô̤ng ua̍h ē mo̍ih-nō̤, cé̤ng náng ga̍u le̤̍h-he̤h, beh-siu̍, táng-hiō̤, boi ē câ̤u, deo be̍h-mó̤ng leo̍h dā̤ lē; bí-do̤̍h Nô̤-a̍ gah deó seóng lē ē, deo diō̤.
24 Cûi dn̂g deó dā̤-sā̤uⁿ, ging-geo̍ seo̍h-bā ngō-se̍h di̍h.

## 8
1 Cṳ̂ ka̍ng-go̍ gi̍-niāng Nô̤-a̍, léng gah i ca̤̍ deó seóng lē beh-siu̍, le̤̍h-he̤h: Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ cuh sâi boiⁿ choi ca̤̍ dā̤, cûi-sa̤̍ ciāng-ciāng te̤̍-kṳ̍;
2 duā-hâi ē sió̤ⁿ-táu gah ting ē pa̍ng deo teh kṳ̍, ting lē ē hō iā suā;
3 dā̤ lē ē cûi ciāng-ciāng te̤̍ kṳ̍: ga̍u seo̍h-bā ngō-se̍h di̍h āu, cûi cia̍ⁿ sa̤u kṳ̍.
4 Chih-go̍ih se̍h-chih di̍h, seóng suā A-la̍h-la̍h suaⁿ.
5 Cûi ciāng-ciāng sa̤u; ga̍u se̍h-go̍ih che̤-ih, suaⁿ-táu cia̍ⁿ hēng chōi.
6 Geo̍ si̍-se̍h di̍h, Nô̤-a̍ kui sê̤ ceo̍ ē seóng-te̤ng:
7 ba̍ng seo̍h táu o-a chōi, o-a boi lí, boi kṳ̍, di̍h-di̍h dêng dā̤ lē ē cûi do̤ kṳ̍.
8 Buh ba̍ng seo̍h táu ba̍h-gah chōi, a̍i-dí kua̍ⁿ dā̤-mi̍ng ē cûi te̤̍ é̤ng ā bōi;
9 dā̤-mi̍ng muâ-ciā ho̤h ū cûi, gah ko̤ beó de̤̍ suā, hói-táu lí seóng ciū Nô̤-a̍; Nô̤-a̍ cuh cheong chiû cī i di̍h seóng.
10 Buh dêng chih di̍h, ca̍i ba̍ng gah chōi;
11 ga̍u bua̍ⁿ ō̤-da̍u, gah hói-táu; chu̍i gó̤ⁿ go̤ng-nô̤-chiu̍ sing ē na̤u̍h: ing-cheô Nô̤-a̍ cai dā̤ lē ē cûi î-ging te̤̍ kṳ̍.
12 Ca̍i dêng chih di̍h, ba̍ng gah cheoh seóng; beó ca̍i hói-táu.
13 Ga̍u Nô̤-a̍ la̍h-bā léng seo̍h ho̍i, ciaⁿ-go̍ih che̤-ih, dā̤ lē ē cûi deo do̤ kṳ̍: Nô̤-a̍ kui seóng-gua̍, kua̍ⁿ-gi̍ng dā̤-mi̍ng ē cûi î-ging do̤ lâ̤u.
14 Di-go̍ih dī-se̍h-chih di̍h, dā̤ deo do̤ kṳ̍.
15 Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ gah Nô̤-a̍ gô̤ng,
16 Dṳ̂ gah dṳ̂ bô giô̤ⁿ, sing-mū, cheoh ca̤̍ duā-seóng.
17 Gah dṳ̂ ē seng-beo̍h, hi-nâ̤u, le̤̍h-he̤h, dôiⁿ-dāng leo̍h dā̤-sā̤uⁿ ē táng-hiō̤, deo dua̍ i cheoh; sâi i seng-e̤̍h ga-ting cīng sā̤ leo̍h dā̤ lē.
18 Nô̤-a̍ cuh gah bô, giô̤ⁿ, sing-mū deo chōi kṳ̍:
19 go̤h-cê̤ng ē iâ-siu̍, táng-hiō̤, hi-nâ̤u, dā̤-sā̤uⁿ ciô̤-gá̤ dō̤ng-beo̍h, deo bíng i ē cê̤ng-lūi, iā deo cheoh duā-seóng.
20 Nô̤-a̍ ing-ūi Cṳ̂ de̤h ca̤̍-dái; ta̤̍h go̤h-cê̤ng tā-gā̤ ē táu-saⁿ, gah tā-gā̤ ē câ̤u, he̤̍ng leo̍h ca̤̍-dái lē, ceo̍ hôi-sa̤u-ca̤̍.
21 Cṳ̂ hiô̤ng ca̤̍ pang-bī, sing lē siô̤ng gô̤ng: Î-hā̤u Guâ dih-dih n̄g ing náng ē é̤ng-go̍, ga̍ng cai-hō̤ leo̍h dā̤, (Ngé̤ng-meóng ceo̍ mo̤̍ dā̤) ing náng ceo̍ sa̤̍ sí-cā̤, sing-guaⁿ ceóng o̤h-niāng; Guâ beó ca̍i be̍h ló̤ng-cô̤ng ua̍h ē mo̍ih-nō̤, chā̤uⁿ î-céng sê̤ ceo̍ ē seo̍h-ā̤uⁿ.
22 Dā̤ ho̤h diō̤ sí-cā̤, geng-ce̤̍ng, siu-síng, ce̍h, chi̍ng, dang, hā, di̍h, má, ṳ̂ng-ê̤ng beó déng-hē̤.

## 9
1 Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ ce̤h-ho̤h Nô̤-a̍ léng i ē giô̤ⁿ, gô̤ng, Seng-e̤̍h cīng sā̤. muâ-muâ leo̍h dā̤-sā̤uⁿ,
2 Dā̤-sā̤uⁿ ē iâ-siu̍, ting lē ē câ̤u, dā̤-sā̤uⁿ go̤h-lūi dôiⁿ-dāng ē mo̍ih-nō̤, léng hâi lē ē hṳ́, deo dih-dih giaⁿ dṳ̂, deo gau leo̍h dṳ̂ ē chiû.
3 Go̤h-cêng ū ua̍h-miā ā̤ dôiⁿ-dāng ē mo̍ih-nō̤, deo seo̍ keoh dṳ̂ sia̍h; Guâ cio̤ng lô̤ng-cô̤ng seo̍ dṳ̂, chā̤uⁿ seo̍ cha̍i-se̤ keoh dṳ̂ seo̍h-ā̤uⁿ.
4 Bí-do̤̍h ū ua̍h-miā ē ne̤̍h, cuh sī ū hā̤ ē ne̤̍h, n̄g-tang sia̍h.
5 Háng ū hāi dṳ̂ miā, láu dṳ̂ hā̤ ē, he̍h iâ-siu̍ ē náng, Guâ dih-dih hua̍h ū ē cōi; náng gah náng sī hiaⁿ-dī, náng nō̤ hāi i hiaⁿ-dī ē miā, Guâ dih-dih hua̍h i ē cōi.
6 Háng láu náng hā̤ ē, náng iā dih-dih láu i ē hā̤; ing Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ ceo̍ náng sī chā̤uⁿ ga̍i ē siō̤ng.
7 Dṳ̂-go̤h-náng seng-e̤̍h cīng sā̤; duā hing-ō̤ng leo̍h dā̤ lē.
8 Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ buh gah Nô̤-a̍ léng i giô̤ⁿ gô̤ng,
9 Guâ gah dṳ̂ léng dṳ̂ hā̤u-a̤̍ li̍h-io̤h;
10 iā gah dṳ̂ go̤h-cê̤ng ua̍h ē mo̍ih-nō̤, cuh sī hi-ná̤u, le̤̍h-he̤h, iâ-siu̍; háng cheoh seóng diō̤ dā̤ lē go̤h-cêng uah ē mo̍ih-nō̤, iā gah i li̍h-io̤h.
11 Guâ gah dṳ̂-go̤h-náng li̍h-io̤h, beó ca̍i ta̤̍h duā-cûi be̍h háng ū hā̤-ki̍ ē mo̍ih-nō̤; iā n̄g ca̍i ū duā-cûi ing ca̤̍ dā̤.
12 Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ gô̤ng, Guâ gah dṳ̂ léng gah dṳ̂ ē seng-beo̍h li̍h ṳ̂ng-ê̤ng ē io̤h, ū mo̍ih-nō̤ ceo̍ bíng-gṳ̍:
13 Guâ ba̍ng kō̤ng leo̍h eóng hn̄g-lî, cuh sī ceo̍ Guâ gah sa̤̍-gang li̍h-io̤h ē bíng-gṳ̍.
14 Guâ sâi eóng da̍u leo̍h dā̤ lē sí-cā̤, eóng hn̄g-lî ū kō̤ng hēng cheoh.
15 Guâ cuh gi̍-niāng Guâ gah dṳ̂-go̤h-náng léng gah go̤h-lūi ū hā̤-ki̍ ua̍h ē mo̍ih-nō̤ sê̤ li̍h ē io̤h, î-hā̤u beó ca̍i ū duā-cûi be̍h háng ū hā̤-ki̍ ē mo̍ih-nō̤.
16 Kō̤ng buh hēng leo̍h eóng hn̄g-lî; Guâ kua̍ⁿ-gi̍ng ca̤̍ kō̤ng, cuh ā̤ gi̍ lē Guâ gah dā̤-sā̤uⁿ háng ū hā̤-ki̍ ló̤ng-cô̤ng ua̍h ē mo̍ih-nō̤ sê̤ li̍h ṳ̂ng-ê̤ng ē io̤h.
17 Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ gah Nô̤-a̍ gô̤ng, Guâ gah dā̤-sā̤uⁿ háng ū hā̤-ki̍ ē mo̍ih-nō̤ sê̤ li̍h ē io̤h, cuh sī ta̤̍h ca̤̍ ceo̍ bíng-gṳ̍.
18 Nô̤-a̍ ē giô̤ⁿ cheoh duā-seóng ē, cuh sī Siâng, Háng, Ngâ-heo̍h: Háng sī Gia-náng ē ló̤-bō̤.
19 Cî so̤ⁿ gá̤ sī Nô̤-a̍ ē giô̤ⁿ; muâ dā̤ do̍ ē bā-sa̍ⁿ, deo sī i ē hā̤u-a̤̍.
20 Nô̤-a̍ kî-chiû ceo̍ chéng, iā cai bú-leó-hóiⁿ;
21 sia̍h bú-leó-ciû cu̍i kṳ̍; te̤̍ⁿ tā-tā, keo̍ng leo̍h da̤̍uⁿ-báng lī-li̍.
22 Gia-náng ē ló̤-bō̤ Háng, kua̍ⁿ ló̤-bō̤ chih-sing lo̍-tâ̤, cuh chōi kṳ gah i nn̄g gá̤ hiaⁿ-dī gô̤ng.
23 Siâng gah Ngâ-heo̍h ta̤̍h i-ná̤u ngiá ga̤ⁿ-táu lē, deo̍-te̤̍ giáⁿ táu-sá̤ⁿ, cia ló̤-bō̤ ē tâ̤; i mi̍ng beó po̤ⁿ-dôiⁿ, deo beó kua̍ⁿ-gi̍ng ló̤-bō̤ te̤̍ⁿ tā-tā.
24 Nô̤-a̍ ciû-cu̍i châⁿ, cia̍ⁿ cai dā̤-dī giô̤ⁿ sê̤ ceo̍ ē seō.
25 Cuh gô̤ng, Gia-náng ā̤ siū mo̤̍ lē; dih-dih ceo̍ nó-cái do̤-ng ē nó-cái, ho̤̍h-seō i hiaⁿ-dī.
26 Buh gô̤ng, Siâng ē Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ da̤u̍h sā̤uⁿ-ō̤; Gia-náng dih-dih ceo̍ i ē nó-cái.
27 Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ sâi Ngâ-heo̍h ē dā̤-dá̤uⁿ kuā-kuā, giô̤ⁿ-seong sā̤-sā̤. do̍ Siâng ē da̤̍uⁿ-báng; Gia-náng dih-dih ceo̍ i ē nó-cái.
28 Duā-cûi î-āu, Nô̤-a̍ ho̤h cāi-sa̤̍ so̤ⁿ-bā ngō-se̍h níng.
29 Nô̤-a̍ ho̍i-siū sia̍h ga̍u gâu-bā ngō-se̍h ho̍i cia̍ⁿ sî.
## 10
1 Nô̤-a̍ ē giô̤ⁿ Siâng, Háng, Ngâ-heo̍h, duā-cûi āu saⁿ giô̤ⁿ, i ē hā̤u-a̤̍ gi̍ leo̍h ō̤-dâ̤.
2 Ngâ-heo̍h ē giô̤ⁿ: Go̤-be̍h, Mâ-go̤h, Mâ-dāi, Ngâ-é̤ng, Tô-bah, Bî-seh, Dá̤-la̍h.
3 Go̤-be̍h ē giô̤ⁿ: A̍-si̍h-gi-ná, Lî-hah, To̤-gia-mâ.
4 Ngâ-é̤ng ē giô̤ⁿ: Î-lī-sa, Taⁿ-si, Gi-dá̤, Do̤-dang.
5 Cî gûi gá̤ náng ē hā̤u-a̤̍, sua̍ⁿ do̍ leo̍h ciu-dô̤, go̤h-náng bíng go̤h-go̤h ē tô-ka̤uⁿ; gṳ̄-go̤h beong ci-pa̍i co̤ng-co̤̍h.
6 Háng ē giô̤ⁿ: Gô-si̍h, Be̍h-sa̤, Heo̍h, Gia-náng.
7 Gô-si̍h ē giô̤ⁿ: Sa̤-ba, Ha̍h-hi-la̍h, Sah-heo̍h-taⁿ, La̍h-mâ, Sah-heo̍h-dá̤-gia. La̍h-mâ ē giô̤ⁿ: Sī-ba, Dâ̤-dāng.
8 Gô-si̍h buh saⁿ Néng-le̤̍h; cuh sī cāi-sa̤̍ kî-táu ceo̍ ing-hé̤ng ē náng.
9 I deó Á̤-Hó̤-hua Cṳ̂ mi̍ng-sá̤ⁿ ceo̍ duā ing-hé̤ng dō̤-lo̤̍h ē náng: go̍-cheô gô-sí ū se̤̍h-gṳ̂ gô̤ng, “︁Chā̤uⁿ Néng-le̤̍h, deó Cṳ̂ mi̍ng-sá̤ⁿ ceo̍ ing-hé̤ng dō̤-lo̤̍h ē náng”︁.
10 I kî-táu li̍h go̤h leo̍h Ba-be̍h, Î-li̍h, A̍-gah, Gah-ní; deo sī deó Sī-ná ē dā̤.
11 Iú Sī-ná chōi kṳ A̍-seo̍h, kî Ní-ní-bí, Lī-hó̤-beh-î-cî, gah Gia-la̍h.
12 Buh kî Ní-ní-bí gah Gia-la̍h do̤-ng ē Lî-seng; ca̤̍ deó sī duā siáⁿ.
13 Be̍h-sa̤ saⁿ Lō-deh co̤̍h, Ang-náng co̤̍h, Lī-ha̍h co̤̍h, Ná-heo̍h-tô co̤̍h,
14 Pa̍-seo-lô co̤̍h, Gia-seo-lō co̤̍h, Gah-to̤h co̤̍h; Hi-lī-seō co̤̍h iú i hiô̤ cheoh.
15 Gia-náng saⁿ duā-giô̤ⁿ Sa̤-deo̍ng, buh saⁿ Heh-co̤̍h.
16 Á̤-bo̍-seō, A̍-mó̤-lī co̤̍h, Geh-gia-sah co̤̍h;
17 Hi-bī co̤̍h, A̍-gi co̤̍h, Sa̤-ní co̤̍h;
18 A̍-uâ-dâ̤ co̤̍h, Sêng-mâ-lī co̤̍h, Ha̍h-buah co̤̍h: î-hā̤u Gia-náng go̤h-co̤̍h sua̍ⁿ kṳ̍ seo̍-chṳ̍ do̍.
19 Gia-náng ē dā̤-ga̍i cé̤ng Sa̤-deo̍ng ga̍u Gi-la̍h, ga̍u Gia-sah; buh ga̍u sê̤-do̤-mâ, Gó̤-mó̤-la̍h, Ah-mâ, Sa̤-peng, La̍h-sa.
20 Ca̤̍ deo sī Háng ē giô̤ⁿ-seong, gṳ̄-go̤h bíng i co̤ng-co̤̍h ē tô-ka̤uⁿ, dā̤-ga̍i, beong ceo̍ bang-go̤h.
21 Ngâ-heo̍h ē hiaⁿ Siâng ā, iā saⁿ giô̤ⁿ; Siâng sī Hi-beh ha̍h-co̤̍h ē cô-co̤ng.
22 Siâng ē giô̤ⁿ: I-lāng, A̍-seo̍h, A̍-ha̍h-sah, Lō-deh, A̍-láng.
23 A̍-láng ē giô̤ⁿ: O-seō, Hō-leh, Gi-tiah, Mâ-si.
24 A̍-hah-sah saⁿ Sa-la̍h; Sa-la̍h saⁿ Hi-beh.
25 Hi-beh saⁿ nn̄g gá̤ giô̤ⁿ: seo̍h gá̤ miá ga̤̍u Hah-leh, (Hah-leh huang-i̍h cuh sī beong-kui ē i̍-gī) ing ca̤̍ sí-cā̤, sa̤̍-ga̍i náng beong-kui; Hah-leh ē nâi-dā̤ miá ga̤̍u Io̤h-dang.
26 Io̤h-dang saⁿ A̍-mó̤-dah, Sa-la̍h, Hah-sah-mâ-hi, Á̤-la̍h;
27 Ha̍h-do̤-láng, O-sah, Deh-la̍h;
28 Gó̤-bah, A̍-bî-mâ-lī, Sī-ba:
29 O̤-hi, Ha̍h-hi-la̍h, Io̤h-bah; ca̤̍ deo sī Io̤h-dang ē giô̤ⁿ.
30 I sê̤ do̍ ē dā̤-ho̤ng, cé̤ng Bî-sa ga̍u dang-bing ē suaⁿ, miá Sa̤-huah.
31 Ca̤̍ sī Siâng ē hā̤u-a̤̍, gṳ̄-go̤h bíng i ē co̤ng-co̤̍h, tô-ka̤uⁿ, dā̤-ga̍i, beong ceo̍ bang-go̤h.
32 Î-siō̤ng sê̤ gi̍ ē, deo sī Nô̤-a̍ so̤ⁿ gá̤ giô̤ⁿ ē hā̤u-a̤̍: deo bíng i ē ci-pa̍i, co̤ng-co̤̍h, bang-go̤h; duā-cûi î-āu, beong ceo̍ le̍h-go̤h.
## 11
1 Do̤ng-sí ting-ō̤ kâ̤u-ing nō̤ seo̍h-ā̤uⁿ, uā-gṳ̂ sio̤ng-dó̤ng.
2 Iú dang-bing buaⁿ kṳ̍, diō̤ Sī-ná dā̤-ho̤ng, gṳ̄-da̤u̍h báⁿ-dā̤; cuh leo̍h hṳ̂-hiô̤ do̍.
3 Go̤h-náng seo gî-leōng gô̤ng, Guâ-go̤h-náng da̤u̍h cá̤ ceo̍ coiⁿ, ē̤ng hôi sa̤u. Bíng ah-na, ta̤̍h coiⁿ do̤̍ng ceo̍ sa̤u̍h, buh ta̤̍h sa̤u̍h-cheh do̤̍ng ceo̍ hoi-tó.
4 Buh gô̤ng, Guâ-go̤h-náng da̤u̍h cá̤ kî siáⁿ gah dái; dái-dêng da̤u̍h gá̤ⁿ ga̍u ting, sâi guâ miá dóiⁿ chōi, mêng dih sua̍ⁿ leo̍h to̤ng-ting-ō̤.
5 Cṳ̂ ga̍ng-líng, a̍i kua̍ⁿ sa̤̍-gang náng sê̤ kî ē siáⁿ, gah dái.
6 Cṳ̂ cuh gô̤ng, Ca̤̍ ce̤̍ng-náng sī seo̍h-ā̤uⁿ ē bā-sa̍ⁿ, uā-gṳ̂ iā seo̍h-ā̤uⁿ; da gi̍-céng ū na ceo̍ lâ̤u; î-hā̤u háng i sê̤ a̍i ceo̍ ē, beó seo̍h hāng ē beó siáⁿ.
7 Guâ-go̤h-náng da̤u̍h ga̍ng-líng, be̍ng-luāng i ē uā-gṳ̂, sâi i-go̤h-náng bā̤ seo-to̤ng.
8 Cṳ̂ cuh iú hiô̤ sua̍ⁿ i kṳ̍ muâ-dā̤: iú ciâ náng cuh beó ca̍i kî ca̤̍ siáⁿ.
9 Ing Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ deó hiô̤ be̍ng-luāng ting-ō̤ náng ē uā-gṳ̂, sâi i sua̍ⁿ kṳ̍ muâ-dā̤, go̍-cheô ca̤̍ siáⁿ miá ga̤̍u Ba-be̍h. (Ba-be̍h huang-i̍h cuh sī be̍ng-luāng ē i̍-gī.)
10 Siâng ē hā̤u-a̤̍ gi̍ deó ō̤-dâ̤, Duā-cûi āu nn̄g níng, Siâng seo̍h-bā ho̍i, saⁿ A̍-hah-sah:
11 Siâng saⁿ A̍-hah-sah āu, cāi sa̤̍ ngō-bā níng, ho̤h saⁿ náng-dṳ̂-giô̤ⁿ.
12 A̍-hah-sah so̤ⁿ-se̍h-ngō ho̍i saⁿ Sa-la̍h:
13 A̍-hah-sah saⁿ Sa-la̍h āu, cāi sa̤̍ si̍-bā léng so̤ⁿ níng, ho̤h saⁿ náng-dṳ̂-giô̤ⁿ.
14 Sa-la̍h so̤ⁿ-se̍h ho̍i saⁿ Hi-beh:
15 Sa-la̍h saⁿ Hi-beh āu, cāi sa̤̍ si̍-bā léng so̤ⁿ níng, ho̤h saⁿ náng-dṳ̂-giô̤ⁿ.
16 Hi-beh so̤ⁿ-se̍h-si̍ ho̍i saⁿ Hah-leh:
17 Hi-beh saⁿ Hah-leh āu. cāi sa̤̍ si̍-bā so̤ⁿ -se̍h níng, ho̤h saⁿ náng-dṳ̂-giô̤ⁿ.
18 Hah-leh so̤ⁿ-se̍h ho̍i saⁿ La̍h-gó:
19 Hah-leh saⁿ La̍h-gó āu, cāi sa̤̍ nn̄g-bā léng gâu níng, ho̤h saⁿ náng-dṳ̂-giô̤ⁿ.
20 La̍h-gó so̤ⁿ-se̍h-dī ho̍i saⁿ Sa̤-lo̤̍h:
21 La̍h-gó saⁿ Sa̤-lo̤̍h āu, cāi sa̤̍ nn̄g-bā léng chih níng, ho̤h saⁿ náng-dṳ̂-giô̤ⁿ.
22 Sa̤-lo̤̍h so̤ⁿ-se̍h ho̍i saⁿ Ná-ho̤̍h.:
23 Sa̤-lo̤̍h saⁿ Ná-ho̤̍h āu, cāi sa̤̍ nn̄g-bā níng, ho̤h saⁿ náng-dṳ̂-giô̤ⁿ.
24 Ná-ho̤̍h dī-se̍h-gâu ho̍i saⁿ Taⁿ-la̍h:
25 Ná-ho̤̍h saⁿ Taⁿ-la̍h āu, cāi sa̤̍ seo̍h-bā se̍h-gâu níng, ho̤h saⁿ náng-dṳ̂-giô̤ⁿ.
26 Taⁿ-la̍h chih-se̍h ho̍i saⁿ A̍-beh-láng, Ná-ho̤̍h, Ha̍h-láng.
27 Taⁿ-la̍h ē hā̤u-a̤̍ gi̍ deó ō̤-dâ̤. Taⁿ-la̍h saⁿ A̍-beh-láng, Ná-ho̤̍h, Ha̍h-láng; Ha̍h-láng saⁿ Ló̤-deh.
28 Ha̍h-láng sî leo̍h bôiⁿ-dā̤-ho̤ng, cuh sī Gia-leh-dâ̤ ē Gó-cî gā̤ⁿ, i ló̤-bō̤ Taⁿ-la̍h ho̤h diō̤.
29 A̍-beh-láng Ná-ho̤̍h, deo ū teô-bô: A̍-beh-láng lāu-mâ miá Sah-lái; Ná-ho̤̍h lāu-mâ miá Bi̍h-gia, sī Ha̍h-láng ē sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ, Ha̍h-láng cuh sī Bi̍h-gia gah Ih-gia ē ló̤-bō̤.
30 Sah-lái bā̤ seng-iô̤ng, beó giô̤ⁿ.
31 Taⁿ-la̍h dua̍ i ē giô̤ⁿ A̍-beh-láng, léng seong, cuh sī Ha̍h-láng ē giô̤ⁿ Ló̤-deh, gah seo̍h gá̤ sing-mū Sah-lái, deo lī Gia-leh-dâ̤ ē Gó-cî gā̤ⁿ, a̍i kṳ̍ Gia-náng dā̤; nō̤ ga̍u Ha̍h-láng, cuh leo̍h hṳ̂-hiô̤ do̍.
32 Taⁿ-la̍h ho̍i-siū sia̍h ga̍u nn̄g-bā léng ngō ho̍i, cuh sî leo̍h Ha̍h-láng.
## 12
1 Cṳ̂ cṳ̄ A̍-beh-láng gô̤ng, Dṳ̂ da̤u̍h lī dṳ̂ bôiⁿ-dā̤-ho̤ng, ching-chih, ló̤-bō̤ ē cho̍, kî-sing kṳ̍ Guâ sê̤ cî-sī dṳ̂ ē dā̤.
2 Guâ beoh sâi dṳ̂ siáⁿ ceo̍ duā co̤̍h, seo̍-ho̤h keoh dṳ̂, sâi dṳ̂ ē miá duā cheoh; sâi dṳ̂ hiô̤ng hā̤u-ho̤h.
3 Ce̤h-ho̤h dṳ̂ ē náng, Guâ dih-dih seo̍-ho̤h i; mo̤̍ dṳ̂ ē náng, Guâ dih-dih mo̤̍ i: ting-ō̤ māng-go̤h ē bā-sa̍ⁿ, dih-dih i̍ng dṳ̂ deh ho̤h.
4 A̍-beh-láng bíng Cṳ̄ ē mīng-līng cuh kṳ̍; Ló̤-deh iā cá̤ giáⁿ: A̍-beh-láng lī Ha̍h-láng sí-cā̤, î-ging chih-se̍h-ngō ho̍i.
5 A̍-beh-láng dua̍ lāu-mâ Sah-lái, gah nâi-di̍h Ló̤-deh, léng deó Ha̍h-láng sê̤ cih ē ho̤̍-cái, sê̤ deh ē nó-bī, deo chōi, a̍i-dí kṳ̍ Gia-náng dā̤; cuh ga̍u Gia-náng dā̤.
6 A̍-beh-láng ging-geo̍ ciâ dā̤, ga̍u Sṳ̄-gia̍ng dā̤-ho̤ng, Mó̤-lī ē chā̤uⁿ-chiu̍. Do̤ng-sí Gia-náng co̤̍h do̍ deó ciâ dā̤.
7 Cṳ̂ hêng-hēng gah A̍-beh-láng gô̤ng, Guâ beoh ta̤̍h ca̤̍ dā̤ seo̍ dṳ̂ hā̤u-a̤̍; A̍-beh-láng cuh leo̍h hṳ̂-hiô̤ ta̤̍ ca̤̍ hêng-hēng ē Cṳ̂ kî ca̤̍-dái.
8 Iú hṳ̂-hiô̤ buaⁿ kṳ̍ suaⁿ-dêng, cuh sī diō̤ Beh-de̍h-lī dang-bing, dō̤ ca̤̍ da̤̍uⁿ-báng, sa̤-bing ū Beh-de̍h-lī, dang-bing ū Gāi: leo̍h hṳ̂-hiô̤ buh ta̤̍ Cṳ̂ kî ca̤̍-dái, kṳ̂ng-giú Cṳ̂ ē miá.
9 Î-hā̤u A̍-beh-láng ciāng-ciāng buaⁿ kṳ̍ náng-si̍.
10 Ca̤̍ dā̤-ho̤ng dû-dû gi-hng; ing hṳ̂-hiô̤ gi-hng cīng tiâng, go̍-cheô A̍-beh-láng kṳ̍ cāng-do̍ Ai-gi̍h.
11 Cio̤ng gṳ̄ng Ai-gi̍h, cuh gah lāu-mâ Sah-lái gô̤ng, Guâ cai dṳ̂ sī saⁿ bā̤u-li̍ ē sîng-ná̤u-náng;
12 Ai-gi̍h náng seo̍h kua̍ⁿ-gi̍ng dṳ̂, dih-dih gô̤ng, Cî seo̍h gá̤ sī A̍-beh-láng ē lāu-mâ: i cuh beoh tái guâ, nō̤ láu dṳ̂ ua̍h-miā.
13 Chiâⁿ dṳ̂ da̤u̍h gô̤ng dṳ̂ sī guâ ē ā-mo̍i, sâi guâ ing dṳ̂ ā̤ deh bíng-ang; guâ ē ua̍h-miā iā ing dṳ̂ deh bô̤-cé̤ng.
14 A̍-beh-láng seo̍h ga̍u Ai-gi̍h, Ai-gi̍h náng kua̍ⁿ-gi̍ng ca̤̍ sîng-ná̤u-náng saⁿ cīng heô.
15 Hah-lô̤ (Hah-lô̤ cuh sī Ai-gi̍h ó̤ng ē ching-ho) ē guaⁿ iā kua̍ⁿ-gi̍ng, cuh leo̍h Hah-lô̤ mi̍ng-sá̤ⁿ ching-ca̍ng i: ca̤̍ sîng-ná̤u-náng cuh siu di̍h Hah-lô̤ ge̤ng-lī.
16 Hah-lô̤ ing-ūi ca̤̍ sîng-ná̤u, cuh ga-bōi hā̤u-i̍ ka̍ng-dāi A̍-beh-láng: A̍-beh-láng cuh ū gú, á̤uⁿ, beô gang ē lṳ́, gah lo̤̍h-dó̤, léng nó-cái, a-táu.
17 Cṳ̂ ing A̍-beh-láng lāu-mâ Sah-lái ē é̤ng-go̍, ga̍ng duā cai-hō̤ leo̍h Hah-lô̤, léng i seo̍h ga.
18 Hah-lô̤ cuh dā̤u A̍-beh-láng lí, ka̍ng i gô̤ng, Dṳ̂ seh-na ah-na dāi guâ? seh-na beó gah guâ gô̤ng, i sī dṳ̂ ē lāu-mâ lē?
19 Dṳ̂ seh-na gô̤ng, I sī dṳ̂-mo̍i? cha dê̤ng-giô̤ⁿ sâi guâ teô i ceo̍ bô: da dṳ̂ lāu-mâ diō̤ ciâ, tang dua̍ i kṳ̍.
20 Hah-lô̤ cuh mīng náng sa̍ng A̍-beh-láng léng i lāu-mâ, gah ló̤ng-cô̤ng ē mo̍ih-nō̤ kṳ̍.
## 13
1 A̍-beh-láng dua̍ i lāu-mâ, gah sê̤ ū ē mo̍ih-nō̤, léng Ló̤-deh, cheoh Ai-gi̍h, sā̤uⁿ kṳ̍ Gia-náng ē náng-ho̤ng.
2 A̍-beh-láng ū gíng ngṳ́ng táu-saⁿ duā sā̤.
3 Iú náng-bing ē dā̤ ciāng-ciāng kî-sing giáⁿ ga̍u Beh-de̍h-lī sá̤ⁿ-di̍h dō̤ da̤̍uⁿ-báng ē ūi-chṳ̍, cuh sī Beh-de̍h-lī gah Gāi do̤-ng, sī kî-táu kî ca̤̍-dái ē ūi-chṳ̍.
4 A̍-beh-láng leo̍h hiô̤ kṳ̂ng-giú Á̤-Hó̤-Hua ē miá.
5 Gah A̍-beh-láng cá̤ giáⁿ ē Ló̤-deh, iā ū gú á̤uⁿ gah da̤̍uⁿ-báng.
6 Ing nn̄g gá̤ náng ē gia̍h-sâng cīng kuā, go̍-cheô beó-dang-dng cá̤ do̍; cio̤̍h-seô cá̤ do̍, ca̤̍ dā̤ beó-dang-dng é̤ng i.
7 Do̤ng-sí Gia-náng co̤̍h gah Bî-lī-sêng co̤̍h, iā do̍ deó ciâ dā̤-ho̤ng, A̍-beh-láng chi̍ táu-saⁿ ē náng, gah Ló̤-deh chi̍ táu-saⁿ ē náng seo-caⁿ.
8 A̍-beh-láng gah Ló̤-deh gô̤ng, Guâ gah dṳ̂ sī ci̍ ching, nn̄g-tang seo-caⁿ; dṳ̂ chi̍ táu-saⁿ ē náng, gah guâ chi̍ táu-saⁿ ē náng, iā n̄g-tang seo-caⁿ.
9 Sê̤ ū ē dā̤, deo deó dṳ̂ mi̍ng-sá̤ⁿ, chiâⁿ dṳ̂ gah guâ beong-kui, dṳ̂ iū-bá̤ⁿ, guâ cuh kṳ̍ cô̤-bá̤ⁿ.
10 Ló̤-deh gio̤̍h-kî ma̍h-ciu, muâ-muâ kua̍ⁿ Io̤h-dāng báⁿ-dā̤, ga̍u ā Sô̤-cî, tó deo sī búi-ceōng. Dng Cṳ̂ bōi be̍h Sê̤-do̤-mâ Gó̤-mó̤-la̍h sí-cā̤, ca̤̍ dā̤ chā̤uⁿ Cṳ̂ ē hóiⁿ, buh chā̤uⁿ Ai-gi̍h ē dā̤ seo̍h-ā̤uⁿ.
11 Ló̤-deh gâ̤ⁿ Io̤h-dāng báⁿ-dā̤, cuh buaⁿ dang-bing kṳ̍. Bíng ah-na, nn̄g gá̤ náng seo lī-be̍h:
12 A̍-beh-láng do̍ Gia-náng dā̤, Ló̤-deh do̍ báⁿ-dā̤ ē gā̤ⁿ, pā da̤̍uⁿ-báng ih-di̍h ga̍u Sê̤-do̤-mâ.
13 Sê̤-do̤-mâ náng ceo̍ gái-o̤h, duā deh-cōi leo̍h Cṳ̂ mi̍ng-sá̤ⁿ.
14 Ló̤-deh lī A̍-beh-láng āu, Cṳ̂ gah A̍-beh-láng gô̤ng, Dṳ̂ iú sê̤ diō̤ ē ūi-chṳ̍, gio̤̍h ma̍h-ciu kua̍ⁿ do̤ng sa̤ náng bo̤h:
15 háng sê̤ kua̍ⁿ-gi̍ng ē dā̤, Guâ bih-dēng seo̍ dṳ̂, léng dṳ̂ hā̤u-a̤̍ ga̍u ṳ̂ng-ê̤ng.
16 Guâ dih-dih sâi dṳ̂ ē hā̤u-a̤̍ sā̤-sā̤, chā̤uⁿ dā̤ lē ē tó-sua seo̍h-ā̤uⁿ: seh-seô náng ā̤ sua̍ⁿ dā̤ lē ē tó-sua, cuh dṳ̂ ē hā̤u-a̤̍ iā ā̤ sua̍ⁿ dih chōi.
17 Dṳ̂ bā-kî, huáⁿ-di̍h muâ-muâ giáⁿ geo̍ ca̤̍ dā̤; Guâ dih-dih cio̤ng ca̤̍ dā̤ seo̍ dṳ̂.
18 A̍-beh-láng cuh buaⁿ ga̍u Hi-beh-leóng ē Māng-lī chā̤uⁿ-chiu̍, pā da̤̍uⁿ-báng leo̍h hiô̤ do̍; diō̤ hiô̤ ta̤̍ Cṳ̂ kî ca̤̍-dái.
## 14
1 Do̤ng-sí Sī-ná ó̤ng Ang-la̍h-hi, Î-la̍h-sah ó̤ng A̍-lio̤̍h, Î-láng ó̤ng Gi-dāi-lô̤-mâ, Le̍h-co̤̍h ē ó̤ng Dá̤-da̍h,
2 ca̤̍ kṳ̍ pā Sê̤-do̤-mâ ó̤ng Bi̍-la̍h, Gó̤-mó̤-la̍h ó̤ng Bî-sa Ah-mâ ó̤ng Sī-da̍h Sêng-peng ó̤ng Sēng-î-beh, léng Beh-la̍h ē ó̤ng; Beh-la̍h cuh sī Sô̤-cî.
3 Cî ngō gá̤ ó̤ng deo hōi leo̍h Sa̤-déng suaⁿ-eo̍; Sa̤-déng suaⁿ-eo̍ cuh sī da ē iáng-hâi.
4 Cî ngō gá̤ ó̤ng ho̤̍h-seō Gi-dāi-lô̤-mâ se̍h-dī níng, ga̍u se̍h-so̤ⁿ níng cuh huâng-puāng.
5 Dā̤ se̍h-si̍ níng Gi-dāi-ló̤-mâ gah dó̤ng-méng ē ó̤ng lí pā Lī-a̍h-ing co̤̍h diō̤ A̍-de̍h-leo̍h-ga-néng, pā So-sa̤ co̤̍h diō̤ Ha̍h-be̍h, pā Î-bî co̤̍h diō̤ Sa-bí-gi-le̍h-déng.
6 Buh pā Hó̤-lī co̤̍h deó Sa̤-cî suaⁿ, ga̍u I-leh-ba-láng; ca̤̍ ūi-chṳ̍ hū-gṳ̄ng ko̤̍ng-iâ.
7 Cî si̍ gá̤ ó̤ng kuáng ga̍u Ang-bi̍h-ba, cuh sī Ga-tia̍h pā A̍-mâ-li̍h co̤̍h ē cé̤ng dā̤, buh pā do̍ leo̍h Hah-sêng-seo̍ng-taⁿ-mâ ē A̍-mó̤-lî co̤̍h.
8 Hṳ̂ seo̍h sí Sê̤-do̤-mâ ó̤ng, Gó̤-mó̤-la̍h ó̤ng, Ah-mâ ó̤ng, Sê̤ng-peng ó̤ng, léng Beh-la̍h ē ó̤ng, (Beh-la̍h cuh sī Sô̤-cî,) deo cheoh-dīng leo̍h Sa̤-déng suaⁿ-co̍;
9 pā Î-láng-ó̤ng Gi-dāi-lô̤-mâ, le̍h-co̤̍h ó̤ng Dá̤-da̍h, Sī-ná ó̤ng A̍ng-la̍h-bi, Î-la̍h-sah ó̤ng A̍-lio̤̍h; cí si̍ gá̤ ó̤ng gah hṳ̂ ngō gá̤ ó̤ng gau-ce̍ng.
10 Sa̤-déng suaⁿ-eo̍ gṳ-do̤ sī sa̤u̍h-cheh-kaⁿ: Sê̤-do̤-mâ ó̤ng gah Gó̤-mó̤-la̍h ó̤ng câu, geong-seō bua̍h leo̍h kaⁿ lē, gí-ṳ́ cuh câu kṳ̍ suaⁿ-lî.
11 Si̍ gá̤ ó̤ng dua̍h Sê̤-do̤-mâ, Gó̤-mó̤-la̍h náng ē heo̍-beo̍h ná̤u-châu kṳ̍.
12 A̍-beh-láng ē nâi-di̍h Ló̤-deh do̍ leo̍h Sê̤-do̤-mâ, siú-de̍h iā dia̍h i, léng châ̤uⁿ i ē cíng-cái heo̍-beo̍h cuh kṳ̍.
13 Ū câu chōi ē náng, lí beo̍ Hi-beh-lái náng A̍-beh-láng gô̤ng, Hṳ̂ sí-cā̤ A̍-beh-láng do̍ leo̍h A̍-mó̤-lî náng Māng-lī ē chā̤uⁿ-chiu̍ ūi-chṳ̍; ca̤̍ Māng-lī gah Î-si̍h-go̤h A̍-nâi ceo̍ hiaⁿ-dī, deo gah A̍-beh-láng geh-io̤h.
14 A̍-beh-láng seo̍h tiaⁿ-mói nâi-di̍h keoh náng dia̍h kṳ̍, cuh dua̍ bíng-so̍ chi̍ leo̍h cho̍-lî chau-lē̤ng ē co̤̍ng-deng so̤ⁿ-bā ih-se̍h-bā̤ náng, kṳ̍ de̤̍h siú-de̍h ga̍u Dāng,
15 léng-má chio̤̍ nó-cái, beong ceo̍ gûi bang, pā ca̤̍ siú-de̍h, buh dui ga̍u Dāi-mâ-seh cô̤-bing ē Hó̤-ba,
16 cio̤ng siū châ̤uⁿ ē mo̍ih-nō̤, gah nâi-di̍h Ló̤-deh, léng Ló̤-deh sê̤ ū ē mo̍ih-nō̤, î-gi̍h hū-dṳ̂ gah bā-sa̍ⁿ, deo châ̤uⁿ hói-táu dôiⁿ.
17 A̍-beh-láng gi̍-céng pā ió̤ⁿ Gi-dāi-lô̤-mâ léng dó̤ng-méng go̤h gá̤ ó̤ng, hói-táu sí-cā̤, Sê̤-do̤-mâ ó̤ng chōi, kṳ̍ Sa-bí suaⁿ-eo̍ cī i, (Sa-bí suaⁿ-eo̍ cuh sī ó̤ng suaⁿ-eo̍.)
18 Sah-lêng ó̤ng Be̍h-gi-sêng-deh, sī ci̍-go̤ Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ ē ca̤̍-si, ta̤̍h ciû gah biâⁿ chōi;
19 ce̤h-ho̤h A̍-beh-láng gô̤ng, Ngōi ting dā̤ ē Cṳ̂, ci̍-go̤ Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ seo̍-ho̤h keoh A̍-beh-láng:
20 Ci̍-go̤ ē Siō̤ng-Da̤̍, gi̍-céng ta̤̍h siú-de̍h gau-hu̍ dṳ̂ ē chiû, gai-dng sā̤uⁿ-ō̤ I. A̍-beh-láng cuh ta̤̍h sê̤ deh ē mo̍ih-nō̤, se̍h heōng tiu seo̍h heōng he̤̍ng keoh Be̍h-gi-sêng-deh.
21 Sê̤-do̤-mâ ó̤ng gah A̍-beh-láng gô̤ng, Náng deo há̤ⁿ guâ, mo̍ih-nō̤ cāi dṳ̂ meô ta̤̍h kṳ̍.
22 A̍-beh-láng gah Sê̤-do̤-mâ ó̤ng gô̤ng, Guâ cî ting dā̤ ē Cṳ̂, ci̍-go̤ ē Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ Á̤-Hó̤-Hua huah-sā̤:
23 Háng se̤̍h dṳ̂ ē mo̍ih-nō̤, cuh nō̤ seo̍h si, seo̍h dá̤u á̤-dua̍, guâ deo n̄g ta̤̍h; mêng-dih dṳ̂ gô̤ng, Guâ sâi A̍-beh-láng cái-cô.
24 Nō̤ ca̤̍ hāu-saⁿ-giô̤ⁿ sê̤ sia̍h ē, léng gah guâ cá̤ kṳ̍ ē náng, cuh sī A̍-nâi, Î-si̍h-go̤h, Māng-lī sê̤ i̍ng deh ē, cāi i meô ta̤̍h kṳ̍.
## 15
1 Ca̤̍ seō î-hā̤u, Cṳ̂ leo̍h be̍h-sī do̤-ng hâ̤u-cṳ̄ A̍-beh-láng gô̤ng, N̄g-sâi giaⁿ: Guâ dih-dih bô̤-hō dṳ̂ chā̤uⁿ díng-bái seo̍h-ā̤uⁿ; dṳ̂ dih-dih deh duā seo̍-ho̤h.
2 A̍-beh-láng gô̤ng, Guâ Cṳ̂ Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ ā, ta̤̍h seo̍h-moi seo̍ guâ; ing guâ beó giô̤ⁿ, nō̤ ca̤̍ Dāi-mâ-seh náng Î-lī-a̍-sah beoh ceo̍ guâ ē hā̤u-seō?
3 A̍-beh-láng buh gô̤ng, Cṳ̂ béng ta̤̍h giô̤ⁿ seo̍ guâ; cuh bíng-so̍ chi̍ leo̍h guâ cho̍-lî ē, beoh ceo̍ guâ hā̤u-seō.
4 Cṳ̂ hâ̤u-cṳ̄ i gô̤ng, Ca̤̍ n̄g-sī dṳ̂ ē hā̤u-seō; bí-do̤̍h iú dṳ̂ cheoh ē, sī ceo̍ dṳ̂ ē hā̤u-seō.
5 Cuh dua̍ i cheoh mói-guā, gô̤ng, Dṳ̂ gio̤̍h gá̤ⁿ kua̍ⁿ ting, ia̍ⁿ sa̤̍u ciô̤-sā̤ chaⁿ, kua̍ⁿ ā̤ sa̤̍u dih lâ̤u bā̤? Buh gah i gô̤ng, Dṳ̂ ē hā̤u-a̤̍ dih-dih iā ah-na.
6 A̍-beh-láng se̍ng Cṳ̂; Cṳ̂ ta̤̍h ca̤̍ sua̍ⁿ ceo̍ i ē gī.
7 Cṳ̂ buh heong-nu̍ i gô̤ng, Guâ sī Á̤-Hó̤-Hua, beh-céng dua̍ dṳ̂ cheoh Gia-leh-dâ̤ ē Gó-cî gā̤ⁿ, a̍i-dí ta̤̍h cî-ciâ ē dā̤ seo̍ keoh dṳ̂ ceo̍ gi-gia̍h.
8 A̍-beh-láng gô̤ng, Cṳ̂ Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ ā, guâ deh ca̤̍ dā̤ ceo̍ gi-gia̍h, beoh seh-na ā̤ cai lē?
9 Cṳ̂ gô̤ng, Dṳ̂ ta̤̍ Guâ ta̤̍h so̤ⁿ níng ē beô-gú seo̍h táu, so̤ⁿ níng ē beô-suaⁿ-á̤uⁿ seo̍h táu, so̤ⁿ níng gang ē míng-á̤uⁿ seo̍h táu, go̤h-cui seo̍h táu, ba̍h-gah-giô̤ⁿ seo̍h táu.
10 A̍-beh-láng cuh deo ta̤̍h lí pua̍ du̍i-bá̤ⁿ, ba̍ng leo̍h nn̄g bá̤ⁿ, sâi nn̄g bá̤ⁿ seo-du̍i: nō̤ câ̤u beó pua̍-kui.
11 Ū o̤h ē câ̤u la̍u leo̍h ne̤̍h lē tṳ́ dē̤ ca̤̍ ne̤̍h; A̍-beh-láng de̤̍h i kṳ̍.
12 Di̍h-táu beoh leo̍h-sua̍ⁿ sí-cā̤, A̍-beh-láng heoh-céng geh keo̍ng leo̍h-míng kṳ̍, gi̍ng-gah duā o-a̍ng, cuh cīng giaⁿ.
13 Cṳ̂ gah A̍-beh-láng gô̤ng, Dṳ̂ da̤u̍h cai, dṳ̂ hā̤u-a̤̍ dih-dih cāng-sí do̍ leo̍h ī-bang; ī-bang náng cha̤-sâi i, bō̤-gio̤̍h i, ga̍u si̍-bā níng.
14 Dṳ̂ hā̤u-a̤̍ sê̤ ho̤̍h-seō ē ī-bang, Guâ dih-dih híng-hua̍h i: î-hā̤u dṳ̂ hā̤u-a̤̍ dih-dih deh cīng sā̤ heo̍-beo̍h cíng-cái chōi.
15 Dṳ̂ dih-dih bíng-ang gui dṳ̂ cô-co̤ng; cuh sī hiô̤ng ho̍i-siū da̍i dńg, cia̍ⁿ geo̍-sa̤̍ bái-co̤̍ng.
16 Dṳ̂ hā̤u-a̤̍ ga̍u si̍ dāi beoh hói-táu ciâ dā̤; ing A̍-mó̤-lī náng ē cōi ho̤h bōi muâ.
17 Ga̍u di̍h-táu leo̍h-sua̍ⁿ ting a̍ng kṳ̍, cuh gi̍ng ū hôi-eng chā̤uⁿ duā ló ē hôi-eng, léng ū sa̤u da̤u̍h ē hôi-iāng, te̍ng ca̤̍ pua̍ du̍i-bá̤ⁿ ē táu-saⁿ do̤-ng ging-geo̍.
18 Dng cî seo̍h di̍h, Cṳ̂ gah A̍-beh-láng li̍h-io̤h, gô̤ng, Guâ ta̤̍h ca̤̍ dā̤ seo̍ keoh dṳ̂ hā̤u-a̤̍; cé̤ng Ai-gi̍h eó ga̍u Beh-la̍h duā eó;
19 cuh sī Gi-ní, Gi-ní-sêng, Gah-mó̤-ní,
20 Heh, Bî-lī-sêng, Lī-a̍h-ing,
21 A̍-mó̤-lī, Gia-náng, Geh-gia-sah, Á̤-bo̍-seō, ciô̤ sā̤ co̤̍h ē dā̤.
## 16
1 A̍-beh-láng ē lāu-mâ Sah-lái bā̤ saⁿ: ū ā Ai-gi̍h ē a-táu miá Hā-gah.
2 Sah-lái gah A̍-beh-láng gô̤ng, Cṳ̂ bōi seo̍ guâ seng-iô̤ng; chiâⁿ dṳ̂ gah guâ ē a-táu ceo̍ seo̍h cho̍; guâ he̍h-chiâⁿ iú i ā̤ deh ā giô̤ⁿ. A̍-beh-láng cuh bíng Sah-lái ē uā.

## 17
1 A̍-beh-láng gâu-se̍h-gâu ho̍i sí-cā̤, Cṳ̂ hêng-hēng leo̍h A̍-beh-láng, hâ̤u-cṳ̄ i gô̤ng, Guâ sī cé̤ng-néng ē Siō̤ng-Da̤̍: dṳ̂ da̤u̍h hō̤ng-seō Guâ, ceo̍ cé̤ng-bī ē náng.
2 Guâ gah dṳ̂ li̍h-io̤h, sâi dṳ̂ hā̤u-a̤̍ cīng sā̤ chio̤ng-sīng.
3 A̍-beh-láng pah leo̍h dā̤ lē: Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ buh cṳ̄ i gô̤ng,
4 Guâ gah dṳ̂ li̍h-io̤h, sâi dṳ̂ ceo̍ cīng sā̤ co̤̍h ē cô-co̤ng.

## 18
1 Cṳ̂ hêng-hēng leo̍h A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng deó Māng-lī chā̤uⁿ-chiu̍ ē ūi-chṳ̍, di̍h-táu de̤ng-gô, A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng dû-dû sē̤ deó da̤̍uⁿ-báng-mói lē,
2 gio̤̍h-kî-táu, gi̍ng ū so̤ⁿ gá̤ náng deó du̍i-mi̍ng kiō̤: seo̍h kua̍ⁿ-gi̍ng cuh iú da̤̍uⁿ-báng-mói lē câu kṳ̍ cī i, pah leo̍h dā̤-lō̤.
3 gô̤ng, Seh-seô mó̤ng-ṳng leo̍h guâ Cṳ̂ ma̍h-sá̤ⁿ, chiâⁿ Dṳ̂ beó cuh lī nó-cái kṳ̍.
4 é̤ng nó-cái ta̤̍h cûi seo̍h deh-giô̤ⁿ, sâ̤ dṳ̂ ē ko̤, léng hē̤-sê̤ⁿ chiu̍-ō̤:
5 buh ta̤̍h seo̍h po̍i-giô̤ⁿ biâⁿ, bô dṳ̂ sing-li̍h, î-hā̤u keoh dṳ̂ kî-sing kṳ̍; dṳ̂ gi̍-céng ga̍u nó-cái cî-ciâ, guâ ing-do̤ng ah-na sio̤ng-dāi. I i̍ng gô̤ng, Tang bíng dṳ̂ ē uā kṳ̍ ceo̍.

29 A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng buh du̍i Á̤-Hó̤-Hua gô̤ng, Cio̤̍h-seô siáⁿ do̤-ng gṳ̄-da̤u̍h si̍-se̍h gá̤, deoh seh-na lē? I̍ng gô̤ng, Ing ca̤̍ si̍-se̍h gá̤, Guâ iā n̄g ah-na ceo̍.
30 A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng buh gô̤ng, Ngōi Cṳ̂ beó siū-ki̍, é̤ng guâ ca̍i gô̤ng, Seh-seô siáⁿ do̤-ng gṳ̄-da̤u̍h ū so̤ⁿ-se̍h ga̤-mńgdeoh seh-na lē? I̍ng gô̤ng, Guâ nō̤ ū gṳ̄-da̤u̍h so̤ⁿ-se̍h gá̤, Guâ iā n̄g ceo̍ ca̤̍ seō.
31 A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng buh gô̤ng, Guâ da gô̤ⁿ du̍i Cṳ̂ gô̤ng, Seh-seô siáⁿ do̤-ng gṳ̄-da̤u̍h dī-se̍h gá̤, deoh seh-na lē? I̍ng gô̤ng, Ing cî dī-se̍h gá̤, Guâ iā n̄g be̍h ca̤̍ siáⁿ.
32 A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng buh gô̤ng, Giú Cṳ̂ beó siū-ki̍, da nō̤ ca̍i gô̤ng cî seo̍h hói; he̍h-chiâⁿ siáⁿ do̤-ng  gṳ̄-da̤u̍h se̍h gá̤, deoh seh-na lē? I̍ng gô̤ng, Ing cî se̍h gá̤, Guâ iā n̄g be̍h ca̤̍ siáⁿ.
33 Á̤-Hó̤-Hua gah A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng uā gô̤ng lâ̤u, cuh kṳ̍; A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng iā hói-táu kṳ̍ ga̍i ē ūi-chṳ̍.
## 19
1 Beoh a̍ng, nn̄g gá̤ teng-seo̍ ga̍u Sê̤-do̤-mâ; hṳ̂ seo̍h-sí Ló̤-deh sē̤ leo̍h siáⁿ-mói lē: seo̍h kua̍ⁿ-gi̍ng, cuh bā-kî cī i, mi̍ng pah leo̍h dā̤-lō̤;
2 gô̤ng, Guâ Cṳ̂ ā, chiâⁿ di̍h nó-cái cho̍-lî, sâ̤ ko̤ do̍-má, dêng ho̤h-nô̤-cô̤ bā-kî giáⁿ-dō. Teng-seo̍ gô̤ng, N̄g-céng: guâ beoh do̍-má leo̍h ga̤-dō lē.
3 Ló̤-deh ca̍i-so̤ⁿ chiâⁿ i, cia̍ⁿ di̍h i cho̍-lî, ta̤̍ i seh éng, geō beó beô ē biâⁿ, i cuh sia̍h.
4 Ho̤h bōi kṳ̍ keo̍ng, Sê̤-do̤-mâ siáⁿ lē bā-sa̍ⁿ, beoh leōng lāu-náng hāu-saⁿ-giô̤ⁿ deo lí, úi Ló̤-deh ē cho̍.
5 Ga̤̍u Ló̤-deh gô̤ng, Ging-má ga̍u dṳ̂ hiô̤ ē náng deó deoh-leo? dṳ̂ chio̤̍ i chōi, láu bíng guâ sing-lî sê̤ a̍i-dí ē.
6 Ló̤-deh chōi, mói goiⁿ kṳ̍;
7 ciū ce̤̍ng-náng gô̤ng, Hiaⁿ-dī, n̄g-tang ceo̍ ca̤̍ gái-seō.
8 Guâ ū nn̄g gá̤ sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ, ho̤h bōi cheoh-ga̍; guâ chio̤̍ i chōi, bíng cāi dṳ̂ seh-na ceo̍: nō̤ ca̤̍ náng gi̍-céng ga̍u guâ cho̍-lî, giú dṳ̂ n̄g-tang câ̤u-tah i.
9 Ce̤̍ng-náng gô̤ng, Dṳ̂ ā̤-gîng te̤̍ kṳ̍; buh gô̤ng, Ca̤̍ náng cāng-sí do̍ ciâ, sī a̍i-dí dī guâ-go̤h-náng lō̤? Da beoh hāi dṳ̂, piāⁿ i ho̤h ā̤ lī-hāi. Cuh de̤̍h Ló̤-deh cīng peh-cheh, hio̤̍ng-céng a̍i-dí pua̍ kui ca̤̍ mói.
10 Nn̄g gá̤ náng cheong chiû tua Ló̤-deh di̍h cho̍, mói cuh goiⁿ kṳ̍.
11 Bi̍ng-chiâⁿ sâi mói-guā ē náng cé̤ng duā ga̍u sa̤̍, ma̍h-ciu o-a̍ng, cheoh-li̍h mo mói, mo bā̤ da̤u̍h.
12 Nn̄g gá̤ náng gah Ló̤-deh gô̤ng, Dṳ̂ diō̤ ciâ ho̤h ū ching-chih lō̤? He̍h náng, he̍h dṳ̂, he̍h giô̤ⁿ-sa̍i. léng leo̍h siáⁿ-lî sê̤ ū ē, 
13 Guâ-go̤h-náng beoh be̍h ca̤̍ ūi-chṳ̍, ing ciâ ūi-chṳ̍ ē náng cōi-o̤h cīng dāng, siaⁿ-ing keoh Cṳ̂ tiaⁿ-mói da̤u̍h, go̍-cheô Cṳ̂ sâi guâ lí be̍h i. 
14 Ló̤-deh chōi, gah beoh teô i nn̄g gá̤ sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ ē giô̤ⁿ-sa̍i gô̤ng: Dṳ̂ da̤u̍h bā-kî lī ciâ ūi-chṳ̍; Á̤-Hó̤-Hua beoh be̍h ca̤̍ siáⁿ. Nō̤ i giô̤ⁿ-sa̍i do̤̍ng ceo̍ lāng-sâ̤u ē uā.
15 Ga̍u ting gng, teng-seo̍ chui Ló̤-deh gô̤ng, Bā-kî, dua̍ dṳ̂ lāu-mâ léng diō̤ cî-ciâ nn̄g gá̤ sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ kṳ̍; giaⁿ lē ing ca̤̍ siáⁿ do̤-ng ē cōi-o̤h, iā cá̤ siū be̍h-mó̤ng.
16 Ló̤-deh ho̤h cheng-éng; ing Cṳ̂ kô̤-léng Ló̤-deh, nn̄g gá̤ náng cuh keng i ē chiû, gah i lāu-mâ, léng i nn̄g gá̤ sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ ē chiû, deo chio̤̍ i chōi, ba̍ng leo̍h siáⁿ-guā.
17 Gi̍-céng chio̤̍ i chōi, cuh gah i gô̤ng, Dṳ̂ da̤u̍h deó-piā, bô̤ dṳ̂ ē si̍ng-mīng; n̄g-tang hói-táu kua̍ⁿ, iā n̄g-tang hē̤ leo̍h báⁿ-dā̤; da̤u̍h câu kṳ̍ suaⁿ lē, mêng dih dṳ̂ iā be̍h-mó̤ng.
18 Ló̤-deh i̍ng i gô̤ng, Guâ Cṳ̂, n̄g-tang ah-na;
19 nó-cái î-ging siū Cṳ̂ ē ṳng, hio̤̍ng guâ duā si cíng-ceó, bô̤-cé̤ng guâ ē si̍ng-mīng; da guâ beó-dang-dng câu kṳ̍ suaⁿ lē, giaⁿ guâ ā̤ gṳ̄-da̤u̍h cai-hāi sî kṳ̍:
20 táu-sá ū seo̍h gá̤ siáⁿ, sa̤̍ buh gṳ̄ng, é̤ng-ī deó-piā; ca̤̍ siáⁿ meh n̄g-sī sa̤̍ lō̤? É̤ng guâ deó-piā hṳ̂-hiô̤, bô̤-cé̤ng guâ ē si̍ng-mīng.
21 Teng-seo̍ i̍ng gô̤ng, Ca̤̍ seō guâ iā ṳ̂ng dṳ̂, n̄g hûi-be̍h dṳ̂ sê̤ gô̤ng ē siáⁿ.
22 Dṳ̂ da̤u̍h gîng-gîng câu kṳ̍ hṳ̂-hiô̤; dṳ̂ bōi ga̍u hṳ̂-hiô̤, guâ deo beó-dang-dng héng seō. Ing ca̤̍ gā̤ⁿ-siáⁿ da̍i sa̤̍, go̍-cheô miá ga̤̍u Sô̤-cî.
23 Di̍h-táu no̤̍ cheoh. Ló̤-deh di̍h Sô̤-cî.
24 Á̤-Hó̤-Hua ga̍ng liú-ńg gah hôi leo̍h Sê̤-do̤-mâ Gó̤-mó̤-la̍h; ca̤̍ sī Cṳ̂ cé̤ng ting-dêng ga̍ng ē.
25 Háng gā̤ⁿ-siáⁿ, báⁿ-dā̤, sê̤ do̍ ē bā-sa̍ⁿ, gah dā̤ sê̤ saⁿ ē mo̍ih-nō̤, Á̤-Hó̤-Hua deo hûi-be̍h kṳ̍.
26 Ló̤-deh ē lāu-mâ hói-táu kua̍ⁿ, cuh be̍ng ceo̍ iáng-tiū.
27 A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng kî-cô̤ bā-kî, ga̍u sá̤ⁿ-di̍h gi̍ng Á̤-Hó̤-Hua ē ūi-chṳ̍,
28 hio̤̍ng Sê̤-do̤-mâ, Gó̤-mó̤-la̍h, gah ih-da̍i báⁿ-dā̤ kua̍ⁿ n̍g, gi̍ng hiô̤ dā̤ ē hôi-heong chē̤ng sā̤uⁿ, chā̤uⁿ á̤uⁿ lē sê̤ cheoh ē heōng seo̍h-ā̤uⁿ.
29 Dng Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ be̍h báⁿ-dā̤ ciô̤-gá̤ siáⁿ sí-cā̤, gi̍-niāng A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng be̍h Ló̤-deh sê̤ do̍ ē siáⁿ sí-cā̤, sâi Ló̤-deh chōi, mêng gṳ̄-da̤u̍h hûi-be̍h siáⁿ ē cai-hō̤.
30 Ló̤-deh giaⁿ nn̄g-gô̤ⁿ do̍ Sô̤-cî, go̍-cheô dua̍ nn̄g gá̤ sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ lī Sô̤-cî do̍ leo̍h suaⁿ; cuh gah nn̄g gá̤ sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ cá̤ do̍ leo̍h suaⁿ-he̤̍h lē.
31 Duā ē sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ gah dā̤-dī ē sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ gô̤ng, Guâ ló̤-bō̤ î-ging lāu leō; ca̤̍ dā̤-ho̤ng beó náng seóng-bíng cing-dō̤, ā̤ gah guâ ceo̍ po̍i-gâ̤u.
32 Hó̤-beoh-cṳ́ sâi ló̤-bō̤ sia̍h ciû, gah i cá̤ keo̍ng, sâi iú ló̤-bō̤ hiô̤ liú-dé̤ng hā̤u-seō.
33 Cî seo̍h má ta̤̍h ciû keoh ló̤-bō̤ sia̍h: duā ē sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ di̍h kṳ̍, gah i cá̤ keo̍ng; i mā̤-lā̤ kṳ̍ keo̍ng, mā̤-lā̤ bā-kî, ló̤-bō̤ deo n̄g gi̍ng-gah.
34 Dā̤-dī di̍h, duā ē sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ gah dā̤-dī ē gô̤ng, Guâ seo̍h-má gah ló̤-bō̤ cá̤ keo̍ng: ging-má cíng-céng ta̤̍h ciû keoh i sia̍h, dṳ̂ iā di̍h kṳ̍ gah i cá̤ keo̍ng; sâi iú ló̤-bō̤ hiô̤ liú-dé̤ng hā̤u-seō.
35 Cî seo̍h-má, i iā sâi ló̤-bō̤ sia̍h ciû: dā̤-dī ē sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ iā dî gah i cá̤ keo̍ng; i mā̤-lā̤ kṳ̍ keo̍ng, mā̤-lā̤ bā-kî, ló̤-bō̤ deo n̄g gi̍ng-gah.
36 Bíng ah-na, Ló̤-deh nn̄g gá̤ sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ iú ló̤-bō̤ huái-īng.
37 Duā ē sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ saⁿ giô̤ⁿ, miá ga̤̍u Mó̤-ah: cuh sī hēng-cāi Mó̤-ah co̤h ē cô-co̤ng.
38 Dā̤-dī ē sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ iā saⁿ giô̤ⁿ, miá i ga̤̍u Bēng-a̍-bî: cuh sī hēng-cāi A̍-meóng náng ē cô-co̤ng.
## 20
1 A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng iú hiô̤ buaⁿ kṳ̍ náng-bing ē dā̤, do̍ leo̍h Gia-tia̍h gah Sṳ-cî do̤-ng; buh cāng-sí do̍ deó Gi-la̍h.
2 A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng ching ga̍i lāu-mâ Sah-la̍h ceo̍ ā-mo̍i: go̍-cheô Gi-la̍h-ó̤ng A̍-bi̍-bî-leh cha̤-kêng náng dā̤u Sah-la̍h.
3 Hṳ̂ seo̍h má ceo̍-ma̍ng Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ ga̍u i hiô̤, gô̤ng, Dṳ̂ ing teô ca̤̍ sîng-ná̤u-náng, gai-dng sî; ing i sī beh-náng ē lāu-mâ ā.
4 A̍-bi̍-bî-leh ho̤h-bōi ching-gṳng ca̤̍ sîng-ná̤u, go̍-cheô gô̤ng, Beó cōi ē bā-sa̍ⁿ, Cṳ̂ a̍i-dí be̍h i lō̤?

# Cheoh-Ai-gi̍h

# Lī-bī Gi̍

# Míng-so̍ Gi̍

# Sing-mīng Gi̍

# Io̤h-sṳ-a̍ Cṳ

# Seō-seo Gi̍

# Lō-deh Gi̍

# Sah-bâ̤u-cî Céng-cṳ

# Sah-bâ̤u-cî Hā̤u-cṳ

# Le̍h-ó̤ng Siō̤ng-ge̤̍ng

# Le̍h-ó̤ng Hā-ge̤̍ng

# Le̍h-dāi Siō̤ng-ge̤̍ng
## 1
1 A̍-do̤ng, Seh, Î-nâ-seō,
2 Gai-náng, Mâ-leh-le̍h, Á̤-le̍h,
3 Î-lo̤̍h, Mâ-tô-sah-la̍h, La̍h-be̍h,
4 Nô̤-a̍, Siâng, Háng, Ngâ-heo̍h.
5 Ngâ-heo̍h ē giô̤ⁿ sī Ko̤-be̍h, Mâ-go̤h Mâ-ta̍i, Ngâ-huang, Tô-bah, Bî-seh, Dá̤-la̍h.
6 Ko̤-be̍h ē giô̤ⁿ sī A̍-si̍h-gi-ná, Lî-hah, Do̤-gia-mâ.
7 Ngâ-huang ē giô̤ⁿ sī I-lī-sa, Dāi-sih, Gi-dâ̤, Do̤-dang.
8 Háng ē giô̤ⁿ sī Gô-si̍h, Be̍h-sa̤, Heo̍h, Gia-náng.
9 Gô-si̍h ē giô̤ⁿ sī Sa̤-ba, Ha̍h-hi-la̍h, Seh-dāi, La̍h-mâ, Seh-dá̤-gia. La̍h-mâ ē giô̤ⁿ sī Sī-ba, Dâ̤-dāng.
10 Gô-si̍h saⁿ Néng-le̤̍h; i sī ing-hé̤ng ga̍i-sa̤̍.
11 Be̍h-sa̤ saⁿ Lō-deng co̤̍h, Ang-náng co̤̍h, Lī-ha̍h co̤̍h, Da̍h-tô co̤̍h,
12 Bah-lô co̤̍h, Gah-lō co̤̍h, Gah-to̤h co̤̍h, (Gah-lō saⁿ Hi-lī-seō co̤̍h.)
13 Gia-náng saⁿ diô̤ng-ceô Sa̤-deo̍ng, buh saⁿ Heh co̤̍h,
14 buh saⁿ Á̤-bo̍-seō co̤̍h, A̍-mó̤-lî co̤̍h, Geh-gia-sa̤ co̤̍h,
15 Hi-bī co̤̍h, A̍-gi co̤̍h, Sa̤-ní co̤̍h,
16 A̍-uâ-dâ̤ co̤̍h, Sêng-mâ-lî co̤̍h, Ha̍h-bua̍h co̤̍h.
17 Siâng ē giô̤ⁿ sī Î-láng, A̍-seo̍h, A̍-hah-sah, Lō-deh, A̍-láng, O-seō, Hō-leh, Gí-tiah, Bî-seh,
18 A̍-hah-sah saⁿ Sī-la̍h, Sī-la̍h saⁿ Hi-beh.
19 Hi-beh saⁿ nn̄g gá̤ giô̤ⁿ: seo̍h gá̤ miá Hah-leh; ing do̤ng-sí sa̤̍-sā̤uⁿ ē náng beong kui; i-dī miá Io̤h-dang.
20 Io̤h-dang saⁿ A̍-mó̤-da̍h, Sa-la̍h, Hah-sah-mâ-hi, Á̤-la̍h,
21 Ha̍h-do̤-láng, O-sah, De̍h-la̍h,
22 I-bah, A̍-bi̍-mâ-lī, Sī-ba,
23 O̤-hi, Ha̍h-hi-la̍h, Io̤h-bah. Ca̤̍ deo sī Io̤h-dang ē giô̤ⁿ.
24 Siâng, A̍-hah-sah, Sī-la̍h,
25 Hi-beh, Hah-leh, La̍h-gâ̤u,
26 Sah-lo̤̍h, Ná-ho̤̍h, Taⁿ-la̍h,
27 A̍-beh-láng, (A̍-beh-láng cuh sī A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng.)
28 A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng ē giô̤ⁿ: sī Î-sah gah I-si̍h-mâ-lī.
29 Î-sah gah I-si̍h-mâ-lī ē hā̤u-a̤̍ gi̍ leo̍h ō̤-dâ̤: I-si̍h-mâ-lī ē diô̤ng-ceô sī Ní-bái-io̤h; buh saⁿ Gi-da̍h, Ah-be̍h, Bi̍h-sang,
30 Bi̍h-mâ, Tô-mâ, Mâ-sah, Ha̍h-da̍h, Dá̤-mâ,
31 Ih-do̤h, Ná-hi-si̍h, Gí-dâ̤-mâ. Ca̤̍ deo sī I-si̍h-mâ-lī ē giô̤ⁿ.
32 A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng ē a-lî Gi-do̤-la̍h sê̤ saⁿ ē giô̤ⁿ: cuh sī Sing-láng, Io̤h-sang, Bî-dāng, Bî-déng, Ih-bah, Sṳ-a̍. Io̤h-sang ē giô̤ⁿ sī Sī-ba gah Dâ̤-dāng.
33 Bî-déng ē giô̤ⁿ sī Î-huah, I-heo̍h, Ha̍h-do̤̍h, A-bi̍-dāi, I-leh-dāi. Ca̤̍ deo sī Gi-do-la̍h ē giô̤ⁿ.
34 A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng saⁿ Î-sah, Î-sah ē giô̤ⁿ sī Î-sô̤ gah Î-seh-le̍h.
35 Î-sô̤ ē giô̤ⁿ sī Î-lī-ha̍h, Liú-cî, Á̤-sī, Ngâ-láng, Kô̤-la̍h.
36 Î-lī-ha̍h ē giô̤ⁿ sī Dá̤-māng, O̤-buah, Sa̤-bo̤, Gia-dāng, Gi-da̍h, Déng-da̍h, A̍-mâ-li̍h.
37 Liú-cî ē giô̤ⁿ sī Ná-ha̍h, Sa̤-la̍h, Sa-mâ, Bî-sah.
38 Sa̤-cî ē giô̤ⁿ sī Ló̤-dang, Se̤h-bah, Ca̤̍-bēng, A̍-ná, Dâ̤-seōng, Î-chah, Dâ̤-sang.
39 Ló̤-dang ē giô̤ⁿ sī Hó̤-lī, Hi-māng; Ló̤-dang ē ā-mo̍i sī Déng-da̍h.
40 Se̤h-bah ē giô̤ⁿ sī A̍-leh-meóng, Mâ-ná-ha̍h, I-bah, Sī-bo̤, O̤-náng. Ca̤̍-bēng ē giô̤ⁿ sī A̍-ngâ gah A̍-ná.
41 A̍-ná ē giô̤ⁿ sī Dâ̤-seōng. Dâ̤-seōng ē giô̤ⁿ sī Ha̍h-beh-láng, Ih-sī-bang, Ih-láng, Gi-láng.
42 Î-chah ē giô̤ⁿ sī Pih-hâng, Sah-huang, Á̤-gang. Dâ̤-sang ē giô̤ⁿ sī O-seō gah A̍-láng.
43 Î-seh-le̍h náng bōi ū ó̤ng guâng-lî î-céng, Î-do̤ng dā̤-ho̤ng ū le̍h-ó̤ng guâng-dī, i ē miá gi̍ leo̍h ō̤-dâ̤: Bi̍-cî ē giô̤ⁿ Bi̍-la̍h ceo̍ ó̤ng; i ging-siáⁿ miá Déng-ha̍h-ba.
44 Bi̍-la̍h sî āu, Po̤̍-seo-la̍h náng Sa̤-la̍h ē giô̤ⁿ Io̤h-bah ciah-ūi.
45 Io̤h-bah sî āu, Dá̤-māng náng Hō-sang ciah-ūi.
46 Hō-sang sī āu, Bi̍-tah ē giô̤ⁿ Ha̍h-tah ciah-ūi, Ha̍h-tah beh-céng leo̍h Mó̤-ah ē báⁿ-á̤uⁿ pā Bî-déng náng; i ging-siáⁿ miá A̍-bī-deh.
47 Ha̍h-tah sî āu, Mâ-seo-lî-gia náng Sang-la̍h ciah-ūi.
48 Sang-lah sî āu, eó-bing ē Lî-hó̤-beh náng Sô̤-ló̤ ciah-ūi.
49 Sô̤-ló̤ sî āu, A-geh-po̤̍ ē giô̤ⁿ Ba-leh-ha̍h-náng ciah-ūi.
50 Ba-leh-ha̍h-náng sî āu, Ha̍h-da̍h ciah-ūi; i ging-siáⁿ miá Bô̤: i ó̤ng-hā̤u miá Bî-hi-dāi-beh, sī Mâ-de̍h-le̍h ē sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ, Bî-sah-ha̍h ē sîng-ná̤u-seong.
51 Ha̍h-da̍h sî kṳ̍. Î-do̤ng co̤̍h ū ca̤̍ co̤̍h-dâ̤uⁿ cuh sī Déng-ná co̤̍h-dâ̤uⁿ, A-leh-uâ co̤̍h-dâ̤uⁿ, Á̤-tiah co̤̍h-dâ̤uⁿ,
52 A̍-hó̤-lî-ba-mâ co̤̍h-dâ̤uⁿ, Î-la̍h co̤̍h-dâ̤uⁿ, Bî-neōng co̤̍h-dâ̤uⁿ,
53 Gi-da̍h co̤̍h-dâ̤uⁿ, Dá̤-māng co̤̍h-dâ̤uⁿ, Bi̍h-sah co̤̍h-dâ̤uⁿ,
54 Be̍h-teh co̤̍h-dâ̤uⁿ, Î-láng co̤̍h-dâ̤uⁿ. Ca̤̍ deo sī Î-do̤ng ē co̤̍h-dâ̤uⁿ.
## 2
1 Î-seh-le̍h ē giô̤ⁿ sī Liú-bēng, Sa̤-me̍ng, Lī-bī, Iú-dāi, Î-sah-gia, Sa̤-bo̍-leóng,
2 Dāng, Io̤h-seh, Bēng-ngâ-mîng, Da̍h-dāi-lī, Gia-deh, A̍-seh.
3 Iú-dāi ē giô̤ⁿ sī Cî, O̤-náng, Sī-la̍h; ca̤̍ so̤ⁿ gá̤ sī Gia-náng náng Sṳ-a̍ ē sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ sê̤ saⁿ ē. Iú-dāi ē duā-giô̤ⁿ Cî ā héng-o̤h leo̍h Á̤-Hó̤-Hua mi̍ng-sá̤ⁿ; Á̤-Hó̤-Hua cuh tái i.
4 Iú-dāi gah i sîng-mū Dāi-mâ saⁿ Hah-leh-seō gah Sa̤-la̍h, Iú-dāi gē̤ng-cô̤ng ngō gá̤ giô̤ⁿ.
5 Hah-leh-seō ē giô̤ⁿ sī Hi-seō-leóng, Ha̍h-bâ̤u-leh.
6 Sa̤-la̍h ē giô̤ⁿ sī Sing-lî, Î-ta̍ng, Hi-māng, Gah-go̤h, Da̍h-dāi; gē̤ng-cô̤ng ngō gá̤ giô̤ⁿ.
7 Gia-bî ē giô̤ⁿ sī A̍-gang, beh-céng tau-ta̤̍h ca̤̍ he̤̍ng keoh Á̤-Hó̤-Hua ē mo̍ih-nō̤, sâi Î-seh-le̍h náng siū cai-hō̤, cuh sī i.
8 Î-ta̍ng ē giô̤ⁿ sī A̍-sah-lî-a̍.
9 Hi-seō-leóng sê̤ saⁿ ē giô̤ⁿ sī Á̤-la̍h-be̍h, Láng, Gí-lō-ba̍i.
10 Láng saⁿ A̍-bî-ná-da̍h, A̍-bî-ná-da̍h saⁿ Iú-dāi co̤̍h ē bo̤̍h-beh Ná-seōng;
11 Ná-seōng saⁿ Sah-meóng, Sah-meóng saⁿ Bo̤-seō,
12 Bo̤-seō saⁿ O̤-beh, O̤-beh saⁿ Á̤-sa̤;
13 Á̤-sa̤ saⁿ diô̤ng-ceô Î-lī-ah, dā̤-dī A̍-bi̍-ná-tah, dā̤-so̤ⁿ Sī-bôi,
14 dā̤-si̍ Ná-dāng-gia̍h, dā̤-ngō La̍h-ta̍i,
15 dā̤-la̍h Ō̤-seng, dā̤-chih Dāi-bi̍h,
16 i-go̤h-náng ē cî-mo̍i sī Sa̤-lô-ngâ, A̍-bi̍-gai. Sa̤-lô-ngâ saⁿ so̤ⁿ gá̤ giô̤ⁿ, A̍-bi̍-seo, Io̤h-ah, A̍-sah-heh.
17 A̍-bi̍-gai saⁿ A̍-mâ-seh; A̍-mâ-seh ē ló̤-bō̤ sī I-si̍h-mâ-lī náng Ih-tiah.
18 Hi-seō-leóng ē giô̤ⁿ Gia-leh teô A̍-so-ba gah Á̤-lio̤̍h ceo̍ lāu-mâ saⁿ giô̤ⁿ; A̍-so-ba ē giô̤ⁿ sī Á̤-seh, Se̤h-ba, Ah-deong.
19 A̍-so-ba sî āu, Gia-leh buh teô Î-hah-dāi ceo̍ lāu-mâ, cuh saⁿ Hō-cî.
20 Hō-cî saⁿ O-lī, O-lī saⁿ Bi̍-sah-le̍h.
21 Î-hā̤u Hi-seō-leóng la̍h-se̍h ho̍i sí-cā̤, teô Gi-le̍h ē ló̤-bō̤ Mâ-gih ē sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ; gah i saⁿ Sa̤-gah.
22 Sa̤-gah saⁿ Gá-cî, Gá-cî leo̍h Gi-le̍h dā̤-ho̤ng ū dī-se̍h-so̤ⁿ ceō siáⁿ.
23 Î-hā̤u Gi-seo̍h náng gah A̍-láng náng dua̍h kṳ̍ Gá-cî go̤h ceō ē siáⁿ, léng Gi-da̍h, gah se̤̍h Gi-da̍h la̍h-se̍h ceō siáⁿ. Sa̤-gah gah Gá-cî nn̄g co̤̍h deo sī Gi-le̍h ē ló̤-bō̤ Mâ-gih ē giô̤ⁿ-seong.
24 Hi-seō-leóng leo̍h Î-hah-dāi Gia-leh sî āu, I ē lāu-mâ A̍-bî-a̍ gah i saⁿ A̍-seo̍h, A̍-seo̍h sī Dá̤-go̤-a̍ ē ló̤-bō̤.
25 Hi-seō-leóng ē diô̤ng-ceô Á̤-la̍h-be̍h ē diô̤ng-ceô Sī-láng, buh saⁿ Bo̍-ná, O̤-léng, O̤-seng, A̍-hi-ngâ.
26 Á̤-la̍h-be̍hbuh teô ā lāu-mâ miá A̍-taⁿ-la̍h, i sī O̤-náng ē ná̤u-lâ̤.
27 Á̤-la̍h-be̍h ē diô̤ng-ceô Láng ē giô̤ⁿ sī Mâ-seō, Ngâ-míng, Î-geh.
28 O̤-náng ē giô̤ⁿ sī Sa-mâi gah Ngâ-taⁿ: Sa-mâi ē giô̤ⁿ sī Ná-dah gah A̍-bi̍-seo̍h.
29 A̍-bi̍-seo̍h ē lāu-mâ miá A̍-bi̍-hái; gah i saⁿ A̍-bēng gah Mó̤-li̍h.
30 Ná-dah ē giô̤ⁿ sī Sa̤-leh gah A̍-be̍ng, Sa̤-leh bōi saⁿ giô̤ⁿ cuh sî kṳ̍.
31 A̍-be̍ng ē giô̤ⁿ sī Î-sī. Î-sī ē giô̤ⁿ sī Sī-sang: Sī-sang ē giô̤ⁿ sī A̍-lái.
32 Sa-mâi ē ā-dī Ngâ-taⁿ, i ē giô̤ⁿ sī Ih-tiah gah Io̤h-ná-dang; Ih-tiah bōi saⁿ giô̤ⁿ cuh sî kṳ̍.
32 Io̤h-ná-dang ē giô̤ⁿ sī Bî-leh gah Sah-sah. Ca̤̍ deo sī Á̤-la̍h-be̍h ē giô̤ⁿ seong.
34 Sī-sang beó dah-bo-giô̤ⁿ, nō̤ ū sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ. Sī-sang ū ā nó-cái Ai-gi̍h náng miá Á̤-la̍h.
35 Sī-sang cio̤ng i sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ po̍i i nó-cái Á̤-la̍h ceo̍ lāu-mâ; gah i saⁿ A̍-ta̍i.
36 A̍-ta̍i saⁿ Ná-dang, Ná-dang saⁿ Sah-bah,
37 Sah-bah saⁿ I-heo̍h-la̍h, I-heo̍h-la̍h saⁿ O̤-beh,
38 O̤-beh saⁿ Á̤-hō, Á̤-hō saⁿ A̍-sah-lî-a̍,
39 A̍-sah-lî-a̍ saⁿ Hi-li̍h, Hi-li̍h saⁿ Î-lī-a̍-sah,
40 Î-lī-a̍-sah saⁿ Sa̤-sa-mâi, Sa̤-sa-mâi saⁿ Sa̤-lé̤ng,
41 Sa̤-léng saⁿ Á̤-ga-bî, Á̤-ga-bî saⁿ Î-lī-sa-mâ.
42 Á̤-la̍h-be̍h ē hiaⁿ-dī Gia-leh ē giô̤ⁿ, diô̤ng-ceô sī Bî-sa, Bî-sa sī Sa̤-heo̍h gah Mâ-lī-sa náng ē cô-hū.
43 Hi-beh-leóng ē giô̤ⁿ sī Kô̤-la̍h, Dāi-bo̍-a̍, Lî-ging, Sī-mâ.
44 Sī-mâ saⁿ La̍h-háng, La̍h-háng saⁿ Io̤h-gua̍ng; Lî-ging saⁿ Sa-mâi.
45 Sa-mâi ē giô̤ⁿ sī Mâ-eóng; Mâ-eóng sī Beh-se̤h ē cô-hū.
46 Gia-leh ē a-lî Î-hah, saⁿ Ha̍h-láng, Mó̤-sah, Gia-seh.
47 Ngâ-ta̍i ē giô̤ⁿ sī Lî-gia̍ng, Io̤h-da̍ng, Gî-sang, Bî-li̍h, I-hah, Sa-ah.
48 Gia-leh ē a-lî Mâ-gia saⁿ Sī-be̍h, Deh-ha̍h-ná.
49 Buh saⁿ Beh-mâ-ná ē cô-hū Sa-ah, Buah-bî-ná gah Gi-bi̍-a̍ ē cô-hū Sī-hah; Gia-leh ē sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ sī Ah-sah.
50 Ca̤̍ deo sī Gia-leh ē giô̤ⁿ-seong: I-hah-dāi ū diô̤ng-ceô, Hō-cî ē giô̤ⁿ, cuh sī Gi-le̍h-á̤-líng ē cô-hū Se̤h-bah,
51 Beh-lī-héng ē cô-hū Sah-mâ, Beh-gia-deh ē cô-hū Ha̍h-leh.
52 Gi-le̍h-á̤-líng ē cô-hū Se̤h-bah ē giô̤ⁿ: cuh sī Ha̍h-ló̤-î, do̍ Mâ-ná-heh, hiô̤ seo̍h-bua̍ⁿ ē náng, sī i ē hā̤u-a̤̍. 
53 Gi-le̍h-á̤-líng ē co̤̍h, cuh sī I-deh náng, Bo̍ náng, Sṳ-mâ náng, Bi̍h-lái náng; buh iú ca̤̍ co̤̍h do̤-ng saⁿ cheoh Sah-li̍h náng, léng Ih-li̍h náng.
54 Sah-mâ ē giô̤ⁿ-seong sī Beh-lī-héng náng, Ní-dó̤-hah náng, A̍-dāi-le̤̍h Beh-io̤h-ah náng, Mâ-ná-heh seo̍h-bua̍ⁿ ē náng, Sê̤-lî náng.
55 Léng do̍ Ngâ-bih hiô̤-sā̤ ta̍h-cṳ náng go̤h cô; cuh sī Deh-la̍h náng. Sī-bî-ah náng, So-ah náng. Ca̤̍ deo sī Gi-ní náng, sī Lî-gah ga ē cô-hū Ha̍h-bua̍h sê̤ dé̤ng-liú ē.

# Le̍h-dāi Hā-ge̤̍ng

# Î-seō-la̍h Cṳ

# Ní-hi-bî Gi̍

# Î-seō-tiah Cṳ

# Io̤h-beh Gi̍

# Si-peng

# Cing-ngé̤ng

# Dé̤ng-dō̤ Cṳ
## 1
1 Á̤-lō-sah-lêng ó̤ng Dāi-bi̍h ē giô̤ⁿ Ko̤-hi-le̍h, (he̍h ceo̍ dé̤ng-dō̤ ē náng, ō̤-dâ̤ iā sī na) i ē uā gi̍ leo̍h ō̤-dâ̤.
2 Ko̤-hi-le̍h gô̤ng, Háng seō hṳ-kang do̤-ng ē hṳ-kang; hṳ-kang do̤-ng ē hṳ-kang, deo sī se̤̍h leo̍h hṳ-kang.
3 Náng diō̤ di̍h-táu ō̤ ló̤-kô ceo̍ gang-hu, ū seo̍h-moi iā lē?
4 Seo̍h dāi geo̍ kṳ̍, seo̍h dāi buh lí; nō̤ ca̤̍ duā dā̤ ṳ̂ng-ê̤ng deo diō̤.
5 Di̍h-táu cheoh, di̍h-táu leo̍h, cīng gîng kuáng ga̍u ga̍i ē sê̤-cāi, buh ca̍i ca̤̍u chōi.
6 Boiⁿ te̍ng náng-ha̤̍uⁿ choi, buh dôiⁿ ga̍u bah-ha̤̍uⁿ; sí-sió̤ng chia-dôiⁿ hiô̤ choi; boiⁿ iú deoh-leo chia-dôiⁿ, buh ā̤ chia-dôiⁿ ga̍u hiô̤ ūi-chṳ̍.
7 Gang-eó deo láu di̍h hâi, hâi iā bā̤ muâ; gang-eó iú deoh-leo láu, buh ā̤ gui hiô̤ ūi-chṳ̍ ca̍i láu.
8 Cio̤̍h-céng ta̤̍h māng-seō dah-de̤̍ng-giô̤ⁿ a̤̍u lí leōng, dih-dih ga̍u duā ló̤-kô; náng gô̤ng iā bā̤ cīng: ma̍h-ciu kua̍ⁿ bā̤ bô̤, hī-kang tiaⁿ bā̤ ce̤h.
9 Î-ging ū lâ̤u ē seō, î-hā̤u dih-dih ca̍i ū; î-ging siáⁿ lâ̤u ē seō, î-hā̤u dih-dih ca̍i siáⁿ, ting-ō̤ bi̍ng beó sing ē seō.
10 Deoh-seo̍h ā̤uⁿ ē seō, náng ū-dang-dng cî, gô̤ng, Ca̤̍ sī sing ē seō le? i ho̤h n̄g-cai ca̤̍ seō diō̤ guâ-go̤h-náng î-céng ē sa̤̍-dāi cuh ū lâ̤u.
11 Î-ô̤ng ē seō, da beó ā̤ gi̍-niāng ē náng, cio̤ng-lái ē seō, hā̤u-lái ē náng iā bā̤ gi̍ lē.
12 Guâ Ko̤-hi-le̍h, beh-céng ceo̍ Î-seh-le̍h ē ó̤ng, go̤h-do ceo̍ leo̍h Á̤-lō-sah-lêng.
13 Guâ ce̤ng-sing ē̤ng di̍-hā̤ gé̤ng-giu̍ ca-míng ting-ō̤ sê̤ ceo̍ ē seō, cuh ā̤ cai ca̤̍ sī ló̤-kô ē do̤̍ⁿ, Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ seo̍ keoh náng, a̍i-dí náng ing ah-na ga̍i ló̤-le̤̍h.
14 Guâ kua̍ⁿ ting-ō̤ sê̤ ceo̍ ē seō, deo sī se̤̍h leo̍h hṳ-kang, chā̤uⁿ kṳ̍ dia̍h boiⁿ seo̍h-ā̤uⁿ.
15 Uang-ke̤h ē, beó-dang-dng ba̤̍h ceo̍ di̍h; pā-kī ē, beó-dang-dng bô heô dôiⁿ.
16 Guâ sing lē ga̍i siô̤ng, gô̤ng, Guâ gi̍-céng deh duā ē gi-gia̍h, buh deh di̍-hā̤, bî guâ î-céng deó Á̤-lō-sah-lêng ē ce̤̍ng-náng ho̤h-ā̤ sā̤, guâ ē sing gṳ-do̤ ū lē̤ng-se̤̍h di̍-hā̤ gah di-sih.
17 N̄g-cṳ́ ce̤ng-sing kô̤-chah, seh-na ga̤̍u di̍-hā̤, seh-na ga̤̍u nguáng-dai, seh-na ga̤̍u gṳ́-cheông: î-hā̤u ā̤ cai ca̤̍ iā chā̤uⁿ kṳ̍ dia̍h boiⁿ seo̍h-ā̤uⁿ.
18 Ing di̍-hā̤ sā̤, huáng-nô̤ iā sā̤; di-sih ga-ting, iu-kô iā ga-ting.
## 2
1 Guâ sing lē ga̍i siô̤ng, gô̤ng, N̄g-cṳ́ ia̍ⁿ-ia̍ⁿ hî-lo̤̍h, meó hiô̤ng ang-háng; î-hā̤u cuh cāi ca̤̍ iā sī hṳ-kang.
2 Guâ gô̤ng lāng-sâ̤u heô-cha̤̍u, Sī gó̤ng: hî-lo̤̍h ū seo̍h-moi iā lē?
3 Guâ sing lē buh ga̍i siô̤ng, guâ beoh kṳ̍ sia̍h ciû, sâi guā-tâ̤ sô̤ng-kua̍i, sing-lî cíng-céng seóng-cé̤ng di̍-hā̤, buh a̍i-dí bô̤-siû gṳ́-cheông, dêng ga̍u guâ ā̤ kua̍ⁿ-gi̍ng náng deó  ting-ō̤, seo̍h-sa̤̍-náng ceo̍ seo̍h-moi seō da̍i  heô.
4 Bíng ah-na, guâ cuh hing-kî duā ē gang-hu; kî ge̤ng-dēng, cāi bú-leó-hóiⁿ;
5 seh hóiⁿ ta̍h, cāi go̤h-cê̤ng ē go̍ih-cî-chiu̍ leo̍h do̤-ng;
6 guâ kui cûi-dí, ā̤ o̤h chiu̍-nó̤ lī-li̍ sê̤ saⁿ ē chiu̍;
7 guâ bâ̤ nó-bī, iā ū saⁿ leo̍h guâ cho̍-lî ē nó-bī; buh ṳ̂ hiô̤-sā̤ gú á̤uⁿ, bî guâ î-sá̤ⁿ diō̤ Á̤-lō-sah-lêng ē náng sê̤ ū ē ho̤h-ā̤ sā̤;
8 guâ buh có̤ng-cih ging-ngṳ́ng, léng go̤h-go̤h le̍h-ó̤ng sê̤ ting-dē̤ng beô-bo̍i ē mo̍ih-nō̤; buh deh cha̤̍uⁿ-geo ē náng-dṳ̂, léng sa̤̍-gang náng sê̤ tang-bō ē, gah hiô̤-sā̤ hó̤ng-ge̤ng ē hi.
9 Bíng ah-na, guâ duā hing-ō̤ng, dah hāng deo ga-ting, bî guâ î-céng diō̤ Á̤-lō-sah-lêng ē náng ga-ting ho̤h-ā sā̤: nō̤ guâ ē di̍-hā̤ cíng-céng ho̤h-diō̤.
10 Háng guâ ma̍h-ciu sê̤ a̍i-dí ē, deo n̄g gi̍ng-cî i; ing guâ gi̍-céng ló̤-kô ceo̍ gang-hu, guâ sing-lî hiô̤ng ang-lo̤̍h iā sī ha̍h-gímuâca̤̍ sī guâ iú ih-cheh ló̤-kô sê̤ deh ē heōng-nō̤.
11 Î-hā̤u guâ cuh kua̍ⁿ guâ chiû sê̤ héng ih-cheh ē seō, léng guâ sê̤ ló̤-kô deh da̤u̍h ē, cuh ā̤ cai deo sī hṳ-kang, chā̤uⁿ kṳ̍ dia̍h boiⁿ, diō̤ ting-ō̤ deo beó lī-ih.
12 Guâ sing-lî cuh dôiⁿ siô̤ng di̍-hā̤, gó̤ng-mō̤ng, gṳ́-cheông: ing guâ gi̍-céng ceo̍ ó̤ng, î-hā̤u ciah go̤h-ūi ē náng ā̤ ceo̍ seo̍h-moi lē? beoh-go̤̍ ce-i beh-náng sê̤ ceo̍ lâ̤u ē cuh sī.
13 Guâ cuh gi̍ng-gah di̍-hā̤ ā̤ ió̤ⁿ-geo̍ gṳ́-cheông, chā̤uⁿ gng-eng ió̤ⁿ-geo̍ o-a̍ng seo̍h-ā̤uⁿ.
14 Di̍-hā̤ ē náng háng seō ma̍h-ciu cing; gṳ́-cheông ē náng giáⁿ leo̍h o-a̍ng: nō̤ guâ gi̍ng-gah di̍-hā̤ gah ngō̤ng ē náng sê̤ gṳ̄-da̤u̍h ē seō deo sī seo̍h-ā̤uⁿ.
15 Guâ sing lē cuh siô̤ng, gô̤ng, Guâ sê̤ gṳ̄-da̤u̍h ē, gi̍-céng gah gṳ́-cheông ē náng sê̤ gṳ̄-da̤u̍h ē deo seo̍h-ā̤uⁿ, guâ deoh-nâi bî beh-náng ho̤h-ā̤ di̍-hā̤? Guâ sing lē cuh siô̤ng, gô̤ng, Ca̤̍ iā sī hṳ-kang. 
16 Di̍-hā̤ ē náng gah ngō̤ng náng seo̍h-ā̤uⁿ, ta̍u-dâ̤ deo beó náng gi̍-niāng i; ing hēng-cāi sê̤ ū ē, hā̤u-lái dih-dih deo beoh-gi̍-dih kṳ̍. Kô̤-sih di̍-hā̤ ē náng sî kṳ̍ gah ngō̤ng-náng beó geo̍-ā̤uⁿ.
17 Guâ hiáng ua̍h-miā; ing guâ gi̍ng ting-ō̤ sê̤ héng ē seō deo n̄g heô, deo sī hṳ-kang, chā̤uⁿ kṳ̍ dia̍h boiⁿ.
18 Guâ iā ga̍i hṳ̄ng deó ting-ō̤ ló̤-kô dâ-ti̍ng; ing guâ sê̤ deh ē, dih-dih láu keoh deō-bôi guâ lí ē náng sâi-ē̤ng.
19 I he̤̍ náng he̍h di̍-hā̤ he̍h ngō̤ng, hn̍g-náng ā̤ cai lē? Nō̤ guâ leo̍h ting-ō̤, ē̤ng di̍-hā̤ ló̤-kô sê̤ deh ē, dih-dih siū i guâng-lî; ca̤̍ iā sī hṳ-kang.
20 Ing-cheô guâ siô̤ng ga̍u guâ deó ting-ō̤ ló̤-kô dâ-ti̍ng ē seō, guâ ē sing cuh sih-mō̤ng.
21 Ing ū náng ē̤ng di̍-hā̤, di-sih, to̤ng-da̍h, gah ló̤-kô dâ-ti̍ng, cio̤ng ca̤̍ sê̤ deh lí ē, láu keoh beh-léng ló̤-kô ē náng ceo̍ gi-gia̍h; ca̤̍ iā sī hṳ-kang, cīng n̄g heô ē seō.
22 Náng leo̍h ting-ō̤ ló̤-le̤̍h, hi̍ cīng sing-gi, ga̍u-bôi ū seo̍h-moi deh lē?
23 I ta̍u-di̍h iu-chá̤u, dah-hāng seō-lâ̤ huáng-nô̤, i ē sing táu-má beó bíng-ang, ca̤̍ iā sī hṳ-kang.
24 Náng gi̍-céng leo̍h ló̤-kô do̤-ng, hó̤-beoh-cṳ́ meô sia̍h meô chōi, sâi i ē sing ang-lo̤̍h; guâ gi̍ng-gah ca̤̍ iā sī iú Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ ē chiû.
25 Nō̤ leōng ga̍u sê̤ sia̍h sê̤ hiô̤ng ē, hn̍g-náng ā̤ ió̤ⁿ-geo̍ guâ lē?
26 Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ sê̤ huaⁿ-hî ē náng, cuh ta̤̍h di̍-hā̤, di-sih, hî-lo̤̍h seo̍ keoh i; nō̤ deh-cōi leo̍h Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ ē náng, Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ cuh sâi i dng ca̤̍ siu-cih hiô̤-sā̤ mo̍ih-nō̤ ē ceh-cīng, î-di̍ gui keoh Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ sê̤ huaⁿ-hî ē náng sâi-ē̤ng. Ca̤̍ iā sī hṳ-kang, chā̤uⁿ kṳ̍ dia̍h boiⁿ.
## 3
1 Ting-ō̤ ih-cheh ē seō deo ū diāⁿ-da̤̍uh ē sí-cā̤, māng seō deo ū ā di̍h-gí:
2 saⁿ ū sí-cā̤, sî iā ū sí-cā̤; cai-ce̤̍ng ū sí-cā̤, ma̤̍h sê̤ cai ē iā ū sí-cā̤;
3 tái sio̤ng ū sí-cā̤, i-dī iā ū sí-cā̤; tiā ū sí-cā̤, kî iā ū sí-cā̤;
4 hâu ū sí-cā̤, cha̤̍u iā ū sí-cā̤; bi-sio̤ng ū sí-cā̤, ta̤̍u-bû iā ū sí-cā̤;
5 diāⁿ sa̤̍uh-táu ū sí-cā̤, kah sa̤̍uh-táu seo̍h-dui iā ū sí-cā̤; peō ū sí-cā̤, n̄g peō iā ū sí-cā̤;
6 cho̍i ū sí-cā̤, sih kṳ̍ iā ū sí-cā̤; ciû dua̍ ū sí-cā̤; ba̍ng kṳ̍ iā ū sí-cā̤;
7 tiā pua̍ ū sí-cā̤, bô ti̍ng iā ū sí-cā̤; beó ceo̍-siaⁿ ū sí-cā̤, gô̤ng-uā iā ū sí-cā̤;
8 sā̤u ū sí-cā̤, che̤ng iā ū sí-cā̤; gau-ce̍ng ū sí-cā̤, hó̤-hô̤ iā ū sí-cā̤.
9 Iú ciâ geh kua̍ⁿ seo̍h-gō̤, ceo̍ gang-hu ē náng ló̤-kô ū seo̍h-moi iā lē?
10 Guâ ū kua̍ⁿ-gi̍ng sa̤̍-gang náng ging-le̍h ló̤-le̤̍h, ā̤ cāi sī Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ sê̤ diāⁿ ē.
11 Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ cho̤̍ng-cho̤̍ māng-beo̍h, deo ū ha̍h sí-cā̤, beó ā n̄g-sī bā̤u-li̍ heô, buh sâi náng ē sing ā̤ chu̍i-do̤̍h sa̤̍-seō; nō̤ náng beó-dang-dng ching cai Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ iú táu ga̍u bôi ē sê̤ ceo̍ ē seō.
12 Guâ cai náng seo̍h-sa̤̍-náng, bí-do̤̍h hî-lo̤̍h gah héng-sēng sua̍ⁿ sī da̍i heô.
13 Go̤h-náng ā̤ sia̍h ā̤ chōi, ā̤ hiô̤ng ló̤-kô̤ sê̤ deh ē, ca̤̍ iā sī Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ sê̤ seo̍ ē.
14 Guâ cai Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ sê̤ ceo̍ ē dih-dih ṳ̂ng-ê̤ng deo diō̤, náng beó-dang-dng ga-ting, iā beó-dang-dng giâng-câ̤u; Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ ah-na ceo̍. a̍i-dí sâi náng gi̍ng-u̍i I.
15 Da sê̤ ū ē, î-cô̤ ū lâ̤u; cio̤ng-lái sê̤ beoh ū ē, î-céng ū lâ̤u: Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ iā sâi î-ô̤ng ē seō ca̍i ū.
16 Guâ buh gi̍ng ting-ō̤ sîng-pua̍ng ē ūi-chṳ̍, hn̄g-lî ū peng-seo; ing-gai héng-gī ē ūi-chṳ̍, huâng ū beoh-gī.
